The living issue list produced by the playtest harness. Newest findings on top. Status: `[ ]` open · `[~]` in progress · `[x]` fixed.

## The harness (how testing runs)

Three complementary layers, because different problems need different tools:

1. **Headless balance/content probes** (`tools/playtest/*.ts`, `npm run playtest`) — simulate hundreds–thousands
   of careers/seasons through the deterministic engine, no browser. Catch *balance/content* issues at scale
   (is skilled play rewarded? is the rival beatable? can you climb the pyramid? are titles winnable?). Fast,
   parallel-safe, run anywhere (incl. Vultr overnight). This is the 24/7 workhorse.
   - `analyze_player_career.ts` — skilled-vs-random policy across many careers.
   - `analyze_manager_career.ts` — dynasty climb up the 10-tier pyramid.
2. **Browser UX agent** (`.claude/agents/ux-playtester.md`, Opus) — actually plays the game in-browser and
   critiques *interface/UX/content-feel* from a fresh-player POV (the layer that found the core card bugs).
   Best run as periodic passes while a session is live (it drives the one Browser pane, so passes serialise).
3. **This backlog** — findings from both layers, deduped + triaged, that we then fix.

**Constraints (honest):** the headless probes are deterministic, so continuous value comes from *sweeping
new seeds/configs* each pass, not re-running identical checks. Browser agents share one pane → they run one
at a time, not truly parallel. True survive-the-Mac-sleeping 24/7 = run the headless layer on a Vultr VPS
(the browser layer needs the desktop app). See [[feedback-overnight-vultr-agents]].

---

- [x] **PT-144 (stage: player Youth/Scholar browser): [low-med] in the summer Step-1 spend screen the "Back the Club" tile is NOT locked/greyed when unaffordable, unlike every sibling treat tile.** Live with 64c left, every other spend tile correctly showed the locked state with an exact shortfall ("🔒 need 16c/66c/236c more"), but "Back the Club" stayed highlighted and clickable; clicking it silently failed with a terse "cannot buy that" line, which then lingered onto the Step-2 focus screen. why it hurts: it reads as a broken control on the one tile that escaped the #7 affordance rework, and the stale error text follows the player to the next screen. fix: route the "Back the Club" tile through the same affordability gate + inline shortfall the other spend tiles use, and clear the error line on step transition. Ref: summer Step-1 spend screen (client/src/main.ts).

- [x] **PT-145 (stage: player Breakthrough/senior browser — observed en route at Scholar): [medium] the "🚪 THE EMPTY SEAT AT EVERY GAME" story arc hardcodes "twenty years of resentment", which is impossible at any age the arc can fire.** The arc's setup says the father "walked out when he was nine"; I hit it at age 15, and the "Agree to meet" outcome read "He sits across from a stranger with his own eyes and his own frown, and twenty years of resentment loosens by a single, painful notch." Six years of estrangement, narrated as twenty. Even at the latest the arc can plausibly fire (Establishing, 25) the true figure is 16. why it hurts: it is the emotional centrepiece of the arc and the one line a player will actually re-read — a number that is flatly wrong breaks the fiction the whole arc is selling. fix: compute the span from `age - 9` and interpolate it ("six years of resentment"), or reword to an age-free phrase ("years of resentment"). Ref: shared/src/storyarcs/relationship.ts (the absent-father arc's meet-resolution prose).
- [x] **PT-146 (stage: player Breakthrough/senior browser — observed en route at Academy): [medium] the between-season money decision serves full senior-professional content to a 13-year-old academy kid, with no age gate.** At age 13 / Academy (turn 12/120) the "A decision off the pitch — money now, or development?" screen offered "The Release Clause — A rival triggers your buyout — a life-changing payday, but you leave the badge that made you (+1,100c)", "Docu-series Deal — Cameras follow him for a season", and "The One-Club Man — Publicly pledge his future here"; the resolution line then called him "a young man". The same screen at age 13 in an earlier run offered "The Loyalty Bonus — A fat renewal to stay put" and "A Magazine Cover — a glossy shoot and a fashion tie-in". A 13-year-old has no buyout clause, no renewal and no fashion tie-in. why it hurts: PT-112 fixed teen fame scaling and PT-134 gated the senior LIFE_KINDS, but this parallel surface still hands a child a pro contract negotiation — it reads as a template misfire and undercuts the careful child/senior tone split everywhere else. fix: gate the offer archetypes by chapter the way LIFE_KINDS now are — child chapters should draw kid-scale variants (a boot deal, a local-paper interview, a bigger club's trial invite), with buyouts/renewals/docu-series reserved for Breakthrough+. Ref: rollOffer / the between-season offer bank (shared/src/offpitch.ts), screen "A decision off the pitch".

- [x] **PT-141 (stage: player Grassroots browser): [medium] the seeded recurring cast (coach/rival) is drawn from a fixed surname bank that includes real bloodline family names, so the coach can share the player's own family name — I saw "Coach Okonkwo" for the Okonkwo dynasty.** Founding a new game as family "Okonkwo", turn 6's Brilliant outcome read "…Coach Okonkwo gave him a nod." — the coach carries the player's own bloodline surname. Root cause: `GAFFER_NAMES` (`shared/src/narrate.ts:22`) literally contains `'Okonkwo'` and `'Ashcroft'`, `RIVAL_NAMES` (`:23`) contains `'Rossi'`/`'Bauer'`/`'Turner'`, and `MENTOR_NAMES` (`:24`) contains `'the veteran Delgado'` — the seeded cast draw (`careerCast`, `:30`) never excludes the user-entered family surname, so a collision names an unrelated coach/rival/mentor after the bloodline. This hits real saves: the existing menu saves include "Ashcroft" (→ possible "Coach Ashcroft") and "Delgado" (→ mentor "the veteran Delgado" = his own name). why it hurts: the family/bloodline name is the game's core identity (whole club, dynasty, and premise are built on it), so an unrelated coach or rival wearing that exact surname reads as "his dad is the coach" or a template mixup — it quietly undercuts the one name the player was told carries down the generations. fix: in `careerCast`, filter the family surname out of the three name banks before the seeded pick (re-pick if the bank collides), or prefix a first name so cast surnames can't shadow the bloodline. Ref: `shared/src/narrate.ts:22-30`.
- [x] **PT-142 (stage: player Grassroots browser): [low-med] a matchday scenario BODY asserts a scoreline/game-phase that flatly contradicts the live MATCHDAY scoreboard on the same card — body said "Two-nil down at half-time" while the board read "Okonkwo's Club 0–0 Marlow Athletic · 29'".** Turn 3's ✈️ Away Matchday 3 rendered the scoreboard widget "Okonkwo's Club 0 – 0 Marlow Athletic · 29'" directly above a body that read "…Two-nil down at half-time with oranges to eat and a team-talk from a dad who means well." — 0-0 in the 29th minute (first half) vs "two-nil down at half-time". The clash comes from the child match-setup bank (`shared/src/narrate.ts:269`), which mixes concrete scoreline/phase claims ("Two-nil down at half-time…", "The school-team game is level…") into flavor that is generated independently of the live scoreboard state, so they routinely disagree. why it hurts: the scoreboard and the prose sit inches apart on one card, so a first-time player sees a plain numeric contradiction (score AND clock) at the marquee "every moment is a story" beat — reads as a state bug. fix: either drop the specific scoreline/half-time claims from the setup bank (keep the atmosphere lines), or feed the actual scoreboard score/minute into those templates so the body agrees with the widget. Distinct from PT-15/PT-107 (context labels / home-away), which are held. Ref: `shared/src/narrate.ts:269`.
- [x] **PT-143 (stage: player Grassroots browser): [low-med] the PT-133 child-cast gate was applied to the play/scenario castReact callbacks but NOT to the life-event resolution one, so grassroots off-pitch outcomes still print senior-tier lines — I saw "His agent breathes a quiet sigh of relief" for a 10-year-old's schoolyard moment.** Turn 4's off-pitch LIFE EVENT (a smaller kid getting picked on) resolved: "He leans on his instinct to share the load… His agent breathes a quiet sigh of relief." The life-event castReact bank (`shared/src/narrate.ts:607-608`) is ungated — `pick(['${cast.gaffer} appreciates how he handled it.', 'Even ${cast.rival} would admit that was well played.', 'His agent breathes a quiet sigh of relief.'])` — unlike the play/scenario castReact (`:467-470`) and charRaw (`:366-369`) that PT-133 wrapped in a `CHILD_CHAPTERS` branch. So the exact senior-tone leak PT-133 targeted still fires through this sibling callback. Compounding it: at Grassroots the meter row shows only ⚡/🧑‍🏫 Coach/🏠 Parents/🧒 Mates — there is no Agent meter surfaced — yet the outcome names an agent, and `${cast.gaffer}` here prints the bare surname ("Okonkwo appreciates…", cf PT-141). why it hurts: it's the same immersion break PT-133 was written to remove, still reachable in the very first grassroots life event a fresh player hits. fix: add the same `CHILD_CHAPTERS.has(chapter)` branch to the `narrateLifeEvent` castReact at `:607-608` (swap to "his coach"/"his mates"/"his dad" framings, drop the agent line for child chapters), matching the PT-133 fix. Ref: `shared/src/narrate.ts:607-608`.

- [x] **PT-113 (stage: player late-career/retirement/heir audit): [medium] every continental cup and World-Finals title the star won is dropped from his permanent legend card / Bloodline Tree — succeed() only banks league titles onto the token's achievements, never cups.** `retireStar` receives `contTitles` and reads `m.wcWins`, and the send-off honour line explicitly celebrates them (`main.ts:1790`), but the succession call omits them: `bringThroughHeir` is invoked as `this.bringThroughHeir(m, seasons, titles, ...)` (`main.ts:1838`) with no cup count, and `succeed()` bumps only `ach_league: (t.ach_league ?? 0) + titles` — `ach_cup` is never touched (`api.ts:429`; the one `ach_cup`-incrementing helper `bumpAch` at `save.ts:209` has no caller). So `legendCardOf` → `legacyCard` computes `cupTitles: 0` and `trophyPts` omits all continental/world silverware (`legacy.ts:67`), understating `legendRating`/`tier`. why it hurts: the Hall of Legends / Bloodline Tree is the game's permanent record of a career, yet a treble-winning icon's node reads "0🏆" and a lower tier than he earned — the retirement send-off praises cups the legend node then forgets, contradicting itself. fix: thread `contTitles + (m.wcWins ?? 0)` from `retireStar` through `bringThroughHeir` into `succeed`'s body and add them to `ach_cup` alongside the existing `ach_league += titles` (pure counter write, no rng — determinism-safe). Ref: `client/src/api.ts:429`, `client/src/main.ts:1838`, `shared/src/legacy.ts:67`.
- [x] **PT-114 (stage: player late-career/retirement/heir audit): [medium] dynasty title achievements read titles from the CURRENT generation's manager state (wiped at each succession), so the Trophy Room shows a lifetime title count beside its own permanently-locked title achievements.** `buildAchSnapshot` sources `leagueTitles: m.titles`, `contTitles: m.contTitles`, `wcWins: m.wcWins` from the per-generation `MgrState` (`main.ts:576-578`), but `bringThroughHeir` calls `clearMgr()` on every succession (`main.ts:1852`) and `loadMgr()` then defaults to `{ season: 1, results: [] }` with no titles (`main.ts:1081`) — so those counters reset to 0 each generation. Meanwhile `seasons` in the SAME snapshot is lifetime (from `api.prestige`'s distinct-honour-season count, `main.ts:580`), and the Trophy Room cabinet renders the lifetime league-title count from persisted honours (`main.ts:2078,2092`). why it hurts: a bloodline with 12 lifetime titles shows "🏆 12 titles" in the cabinet while "A Dynasty Forms (3)" / "Kings of the League (10)" sit LOCKED right below it because the current heir has only won a few — an on-screen contradiction that guts the multi-generational dynasty payoff those very achievements are themed around. fix: in `buildAchSnapshot`, derive `leagueTitles`/`contTitles`/`wcWins` from lifetime honours (league = `honours.filter(h => h.title===1 && kind==='league')`, cont/world by `kind`) the way the cabinet does, instead of the per-generation `MgrState` (presentational, determinism-safe). Ref: `client/src/main.ts:576-580`, `client/src/main.ts:2078`.
- [x] **PT-115 (stage: player late-career/retirement/heir audit): [low-med] the entire retired-player legend keepsake card + "✦ Reborn — breed the next generation" button in showPlayerCard is unreachable dead code in single-player — no token ever enters the 'retired' state.** `showPlayerCard`'s `ci.retired` branch renders a legend keepsake (rating/titles/apps) and the 150c Reborn button (`main.ts:740-746`), gated on `tokenContract().retired`, which is true only when `t.state === 'retired'` (`tokens.ts:73`). The only writer of that state, `localStore.retirePlayer` (`save.ts:195`), has NO caller anywhere in the client — the succession flow (`succeed`) transitions the token pro→prospect directly via `rebornFields`, never through 'retired'. So the branch, its `api.reborn` handler (`api.ts:392`), `rebornPlayer` (`main.ts:783`), and the `legend`/`rebornId` contract fields (`api.ts:275`) are all permanently dead. why it hurts: it's a stale second, competing "breed the next generation" surface (a superseded NFT-era path) carried in the shipped code — dead weight that can drift out of sync with the real succession UX and confuses anyone auditing the legacy loop. fix: either delete the `ci.retired` branch + `reborn`/`rebornPlayer`/`retirePlayer` path, or, if a "view a past legend's card" surface is wanted, wire it to the persisted `legacies` store instead of the never-set 'retired' token state. Ref: `client/src/main.ts:740-746`, `client/src/save.ts:195`, `client/src/api.ts:392`.

- [x] **PT-116 (stage: player late-career/retirement/heir audit): [low-med] the legacy card's `testimonial` coin send-off is computed (up to 2000c) but never paid at succession nor shown anywhere — a documented retirement payout is dead.** `legacyCard` computes `testimonial = clamp(round(legendRating * 6 * (1 + highestTierIdx*0.08)), 0, 2000)` and the module header states it "pays a bounded coin send-off" (`legacy.ts:4,73`). But `succeed()` credits only `legacy` (earnings × 0.6, optionally ×1.75 for the Fortune will) and `saleFee` (`api.ts:440-447`) — it never reads `card.testimonial` — and the Trophy Room / Bloodline Tree render legendRating/tier/titles/apps/seasons but never `testimonial` (nor `mintable`), so the field is neither paid nor displayed. why it hurts: a testimonial is a signature real-football retirement beat the code explicitly models and then silently discards, so a great servant's send-off has no tangible reward and the comment misleads future work. fix: either pay `legendCardOf(decorated).testimonial` into coins at succession (a bounded one-off, greatness-scaled — determinism-safe) and surface it on the send-off screen, or drop the dead `testimonial` field + its comment to stop implying a payout that never happens. Ref: `shared/src/legacy.ts:73`, `client/src/api.ts:440`.

- [x] **PT-101 [FIXED] (stage: player Grassroots re-pass): [medium] outcome-prose persona/trait flavour lines recycle verbatim within the first 6 Grassroots turns — three separate lines repeated, one back-to-back.** Across turns 1–6 of one fresh sitting: "Even the opposition applauded." closed BOTH the turn-1 and turn-2 outcomes (consecutive); "The engine simply doesn't cut out." closed the turn-2 and turn-4 outcomes; and the opener "Past a car park doubling as the changing rooms," began BOTH the turn-2 ("…he dared to try Stepover and grown adults gasped") and turn-6 ("…he, lungs burning, conjured Defence-Splitter") outcomes. why it hurts: the "every moment is its own story" hook is the whole pitch of a text game, and a first-time player sees the identical stock sentence twice inside their first six turns (once back-to-back) — the template seam shows immediately at the make-or-break first impression. fix: give the Grassroots OUTCOME flavour bank (opener + persona/trait closer) a larger pool and track recently-used lines so no line repeats within a stage/short window (same treatment PT-54 asked for the seasonEvent openers, but for the per-turn outcome banks). Ref: outcome narration banks in `shared/src/narrate.ts`.
- [x] **PT-102 [FIXED] (stage: player Grassroots re-pass): [medium] the persona-trait outcome closer is tone-blind — a POSITIVE trait line is appended to a POOR/failed outcome, praising the player for the moment that just fell apart.** On turn 4 I deliberately played the wrong card; the graded-Poor outcome read "On a muddy park pitch, he, for the fifth time that half, crunched into Crunching Tackle and it fell apart completely. You could hear a pin drop on the bench. **The engine simply doesn't cut out.**" — the closer ("engine doesn't cut out", Rico's "boundless energy" persona line, seen as praise on the Brilliant turn 2) is glued onto a failure with no tone branch, so the failure ends on a compliment. why it hurts: it undercuts the one negative-feedback beat a new player gets — the prose says "it fell apart" then immediately praises him — muddying whether the wrong card was actually bad. fix: branch the persona/trait closer bank on grade (only append the flattering trait line on Right/Brilliant; use a deflating or neutral trait callback on Partial/Poor). Ref: outcome narration (grade-agnostic trait closer) in `shared/src/narrate.ts`.

(turn 6) — a subs bench, a "ground", and an applauding opposition read as academy/senior-match staging, not a cold park pitch with a dad on the touchline. why it hurts: PT-46 tuned the age-tone but these venue/crowd words in the outcome bank still nudge Grassroots toward big-match language, diluting the deliberately humble "muddy knees and big dreams" frame at the very first stage. fix: swap crowd/venue nouns in the Grassroots outcome bank for park equivalents (touchline, the far side, the handful of parents) or gate them to later stages. Ref: Grassroots outcome flavour bank in `shared/src/narrate.ts`.

- [x] **PT-98 [FIXED] (stage: regression re-pass): [medium] on the title screen the game's own logo is scrolled off the top of the viewport on load — the first paint shows a big empty band above the buttons instead of the wordmark.** On a fresh load of the menu at the 110% default with the save list populated (8 saves), `renderMainMenu` calls `focus()` on the Continue button, which auto-scrolls the tall page (docHeight ~1441 vs viewport) — measured `window.scrollY ≈ 633`, `#mm-emblem` (the crown+ball + "FOOTBALL ROYALTY" wordmark) at `top ≈ -434px`, fully above the fold; the player must scroll UP to see the title. Reproduces on every reload (activeElement = `mm-continue`). why it hurts: the hero/branding — the first thing a new player should see — is invisible at load, and the empty dark band above the buttons reads as a broken/half-loaded page at exactly the 110% default the pass was told to sanity-check. fix: pre-focus with `focusBtn.focus({ preventScroll: true })` (and/or `window.scrollTo(0,0)` after render) so the emblem stays in view. Ref: `client/src/main.ts` renderMainMenu focus() ~:363.
- [x] **PT-99 [FIXED] (stage: regression re-pass): [low-med] New Game accepts a family name identical to an existing save with no guard, producing two saves that are indistinguishable in the Switch Save list.** `startNewGame` derives the save id as `name+"-"+random4` and pushes `{name}` with no uniqueness check (`client/src/main.ts:511-517`); the switcher row renders only `name` + `toLocaleDateString()` (no time) (`:361`), so two saves both named e.g. "Delacroix" created the same day show as identical rows — "Delacroix · 2026. 8. 29." twice — and the delete confirm quotes the same name for both. why it hurts: a player who reuses a family name (very likely for a "second attempt at the Messi dynasty") can't tell the two bloodlines apart in the switcher and can load or permanently delete the wrong one. fix: warn on New Game when the cleaned name matches an existing save ("You already have a Delacroix save — start another?"), and/or disambiguate rows (show time, or a "#2"/generation/tier tag). Ref: `client/src/main.ts:361,509-517`.
- [x] **PT-100 [FIXED] (stage: regression re-pass): [low] the family-name title-caser only capitalises the first letter of each space-separated word, so letters after an apostrophe or hyphen stay lowercase — real surnames render wrong.** `cleanFamilyName` does `.split(' ').map(w => w[0].toUpperCase()+w.slice(1).toLowerCase())` (`client/src/main.ts:47-48`), which handles spaces (PT-80) but not intra-word separators: live preview typed "o'brien-smith" → "O'brien-smith's Club · the O'brien-smith bloodline" (should be "O'Brien-Smith"); "macdonald" is fine but "d'angelo"→"D'angelo", "jones-smith"→"Jones-smith". why it hurts: apostrophe/hyphen surnames are common and this is the one name the player chooses and sees carried across the whole dynasty, so a mis-cased family name reads as sloppy at the identity beat. fix: capitalise the letter after any word-boundary including `'` and `-`, e.g. title-case on `/([ '\-])/` segments (or a `\b`-aware replace). Ref: `client/src/main.ts:46-48` cleanFamilyName.

- [x] **PT-55 [FIXED] (stage: player late-career/heir re-pass): [high] the dynasty's family name is SPLIT IN TWO — the club/bloodline uses the name you typed at New Game ("Marchetti"), but your actual player-character + every heir carries a random surname ("Sato"), and the succession prose keys off the random one.** LIVE on the Marchetti save the hub reads "Marchetti's Club · the Marchetti bloodline" up top while YOUR PLAYER is "Leo Sato · gen 1 · pedigree 70%"; cross-checked on the Okonkwo save ("Okonkwo's Club" but player "Jude Cruz"). Root cause: New Game stores the typed name only as the CLUB name (`client/src/api.ts:209`, `register`), while the scouted prospect/founder token is named `nameFor(seed)` — a random FIRST+LAST bank pick, independent of the club (`client/src/api.ts:179,253`; `shared/src/tokens.ts:29-31`); `rebornFields` then carries that RANDOM surname down the generations (`shared/src/tokens.ts:392-396`). So `retireStar` computes the succession surname from `m.starName` → the epilogue literally says "But the **Sato** name isn't done — his son is already coming through the youth ranks" directly beneath "Marchetti's Club" (`client/src/main.ts:1696,1708`). why it hurts: the entire pitch is "found a bloodline — the family name carries down the generations", and it's the one thing the player is asked to choose at New Game (PT-39/40/41), yet the name they choose is NEVER the name their footballing family actually bears; the payoff beat celebrates a surname the player never picked and doesn't recognise · fix: either name the founder prospect after the chosen bloodline name (surname = the New-Game name, random first name from the bank) so club + player + heirs all share one family name, or let the player name the founding PLAYER (not just the club) and derive the club label from that. Ref: `client/src/api.ts:179,209,253`, `shared/src/tokens.ts:29-31,392-396`, `client/src/main.ts:1696,1708` (live: Marchetti hub + Okonkwo hub).
- [x] **PT-56 [FIXED] (stage: player late-career/heir re-pass): [medium] (audit-based) the "What does he do next?" succession sub-choice is BLIND and a hidden fake-choice — the three cards render with no description until AFTER you commit, and only "mentor the heir" applies a mechanical bonus (coaching/media silently give the heir nothing).** In `retireStar` the NEXT_LIFE cards are drawn as icon+label ONLY — `<div class="cg-cname">${nl.icon} ${nl.label}</div>` with no `cg-cdesc` (`client/src/main.ts:1710-1713`) — so the evocative blurbs ("A studio wants him behind a microphone next season…") appear only once you've chosen (`:1724`); yet the very next screen, THE WILL, shows every card's blurb up front (`:1722`). Worse, only `mentoring` banks the veteran-years bonus: `appliedMentorship = choice === 'mentoring' ? mentorship : 0` (`:1717`), and the "🎓 mentored heir (+N mentality)" reward line only prints for that pick (`:1718`) — a player who picks Coaching or Media forfeits a real composure/leadership head-start for their heir (`client/src/api.ts:445`) with zero on-screen signal that Mentor was the "help your heir" option. why it hurts: at the game's most emotional decision the player chooses blind between three identical-looking cards, and the "wrong" two quietly cost the next generation a development bonus — the same hidden-tradeoff pattern flagged for contracts (PT-32/33), here at the marquee succession beat · fix: render each NEXT_LIFE card's blurb up front like the WILL cards, and surface the heir consequence on the cards ("🧭 Mentor — the only path that passes his experience to the heir") so the choice is informed. Ref: `client/src/main.ts:1666-1669,1710-1718`, `client/src/api.ts:445`.
- [x] **PT-57 [FIXED] (stage: player late-career/heir re-pass): [medium] (audit-based) the RETIREMENT send-off — the dynasty's emotional peak — is a single flat template with no personality/cast flavour and zero run-to-run variation, so it reads MORE generic than the lesser age-25 graduation epilogue.** `retireStar`'s `storyLine` is one hardcoded sentence with interpolated names/counts only ("… once the heartbeat of this side on the pitch, latterly the man in the dugout — retires a club great after N seasons…"), no `pickFrom`, no persona line, no `careerCast` gaffer/mentor/rival callbacks (`client/src/main.ts:1703-1708`). By contrast the AGE-25 `graduationEpilogue` — a mid-career beat — has multi-variant open/close banks, a per-personality closing line (14 variants: "Nobody outworked him. Nobody ever will." etc.) and cast callbacks (`shared/src/narrate.ts:781-816`). So the same player's FINAL farewell is less characterful and identical every save, while his graduation years earlier was bespoke. why it hurts: the retirement/succession is the whole "bloodline dynasty" payoff and the one moment meant to feel most personal, yet it's the most template-y prose in the career — and a returning player who has graduated several bloodlines will have read the exact same retirement words every time · fix: give retirement its own `retirementEpilogue()` in narrate.ts with the same treatment as `graduationEpilogue` (personality line keyed off `t.personality`, cast callbacks, seeded multi-variant open/close) and pull his actual playing legacy (peak_overall / caps / legend rating) into it. Ref: `client/src/main.ts:1690-1708`, `shared/src/narrate.ts:779-816`.
- [x] **PT-58 [FIXED] (stage: player late-career/heir re-pass): [low-med] the succession copy calls the heir "his son … already coming through the youth ranks", but the heir is actually a brand-new age-10 Grassroots prospect you develop from scratch — and "son" hardcodes gender.** The send-off says "But the {surname} name isn't done — **his son is already coming through the youth ranks**" (`client/src/main.ts:1708`), yet `rebornFields` produces a fresh gen+1 PROSPECT with `career_seed: null` / `state: 'prospect'` (`shared/src/tokens.ts:396-404`) — LIVE the heir shows on the hub as "Leo Sato · gen 1 · **age 10, ready to develop**" at Academy, i.e. a 10-year-old at the very START of the 10→25 career, not someone "coming through the youth ranks" (a later chapter). why it hurts: it over-sells where the heir is (the player expects a near-ready youth-teamer and instead gets a kid to raise from Grassroots), and the fixed "his son" closes off ever having a daughter in a family life-sim · fix: reword to match the real state ("a new {surname} is about to kick a ball for the first time" / "the next of the line is starting out"), and neutralise the gendered "son" (e.g. "the next of the {surname} line"). Ref: `client/src/main.ts:1708`, `shared/src/tokens.ts:396-404` (live: Marchetti heir hub).
- [x] **PT-59 (stage: player late-career/heir re-pass): [low] a save that completed its succession BEFORE the PT-18 legend-snapshot fix is left permanently self-contradicting, with no backfill — distinct from PT-18 (the fix itself).** LIVE the Marchetti save has fully succeeded (gen-2 heir active, "🌳 The Line Continues" UNLOCKED) yet the Trophy Room reads "🌳 0 bloodlines · ⭐ 0 legends", the BLOODLINES panel shows the empty-state ("No bloodlines yet — develop a player… retire him to found a dynasty"), and "🔒 A Legend Retires" sits LOCKED directly beside the unlocked "🌳 The Line Continues" — the exact contradiction PT-18 fixed for NEW successions. Because `saveLegacy` only runs at `api.succeed` time (`client/src/api.ts:434-437`), pre-fix saves whose founder already retired never get a legend node and there's no migration to reconstruct one. why it hurts: existing dynasties (the most-invested players) keep a visibly broken Hall of Legends / Bloodline Tree forever — the game's signature generational surface stays empty for the people furthest into it · fix: on load (or on entering the Trophy Room), if a token is `state:'prospect'` with `generation >= 1` and no legend node exists for its prior generations, backfill a legend snapshot from the retained achievement fields so the tree/hall reflect successions that predate the fix. Ref: `client/src/api.ts:431-437` (saveLegacy at succeed only), `client/src/main.ts` showTrophyRoom (live: Marchetti Trophy Room).

- [x] **PT-60 [FIXED] (audit: manager transfers/contracts): [high] accepting an incoming bid banks the fee BEFORE the abandonable succession that removes the star — repeatable free-money loop + "sold but still owned" state.** `acceptStarBid` calls `api.sellStar(bid.fee)` (only `addCoins`, api.ts:363-367, main.ts:1682-1685); the star isn't retired/reborn until `api.succeed`, which fires only after the multi-step next-life→will screens complete (`bringThroughHeir`, main.ts:1740). Abandon the will screen or reload → coins kept, star still 'pro' in the squad, re-offered next season → sell again. why it hurts: repeatable coin exploit + inconsistent save (double-process/soft-lock class). fix: bank the fee inside `api.succeed` (make it atomic with sellStar), or set a "sale pending" flag on the token in sellStar so a re-render/reload can't re-offer or re-sell. Ref: client/src/api.ts:363, client/src/main.ts:1682.
- [x] **PT-61 [FIXED] (audit: manager transfers/contracts): [medium] contract-accept toast understates the spend by ×length.** On accept, `api.negotiateStar` charges `wage × L` (api.ts:353-356) but the success toast prints only the per-season wage (`−${wage}c`, main.ts:922) — a 5-yr deal at 5,000c/season deducts 25,000c but reads "−5,000c". fix: show `−${(wage*length)}c` (the total) in the toast, matching the modal. Ref: client/src/main.ts:922.
- [x] **PT-62 [FIXED] (audit: manager transfers/contracts): [medium] the marquee incoming-bid fee is economically trivial.** `incomingBid` bases the offer on `sellValue` (the offload-haircut) ×premium×variance (transfermarket.ts:60-63), so an OV16-18 star fetches ~1,000-1,200c — barely above a bench signing, while re-signing him costs thousands. why it hurts: the "cash in the bloodline star vs keep him" drama is hollow and lopsided. fix: base the bid on releaseClause/transferFee (a premium over buy-price), not the sellValue haircut. Ref: shared/src/transfermarket.ts:60.
- [x] **PT-63 [FIXED] (audit: manager transfers/contracts): [low-med] contract-talks modal shows neither coins nor affordability — an unaffordable offer only fails after clicking.** The negotiation render (main.ts:891-910) never shows the coin balance and never disables offers whose `wage×length` exceeds coins; `negotiateStar` throws 402 on submit (api.ts:355). Mirrors PT-31 (transfer market got coins+disabled-reasons) but the contract modal was left without. fix: show coins in cn-body + disable/annotate unaffordable offers. Ref: client/src/main.ts:891.
- [x] **PT-64 [FIXED] (audit: manager promotion/relegation): [medium] board "This season they expect: X" is one season stale.** `nextSeason` derives `expectation` from `m.lastFinishPos` (the season BEFORE the one just ended) into `lastBoard` (main.ts:1618,1624,1647); `showSeason` prints it as the COMING season's target (main.ts:1263). A just-promoted/relegated club sees the pre-move expectation on its new-tier screen. fix: recompute the upcoming expectation from the just-finished `t.pos` and store THAT as lastBoard.expectation. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1618.
- [x] **PT-65 [FIXED] (audit: manager promotion/relegation): [medium] "playoffs" is shown as a board expectation, but the pyramid has no playoffs (pure top-2 auto-promotion).** `deriveExpectation` returns 'playoffs' and `finishOf` returns 'playoffs' for pos≤3 (board.ts:167, main.ts:1657), rendered verbatim as "expect: playoffs" (main.ts:1263); league promotes only top 2 (main.ts:1633). fix: humanize the enum for display ('playoffs'→"a top-half push") and don't surface raw enum strings. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1263.
- [x] **PT-66 [FIXED] (audit: manager promotion/relegation): [medium] finishOf never emits 'promotion'/'relegated', so deriveExpectation's promotion(+1)/relegated(−2) nudges are dead code.** `finishOf` returns only null/'title'/'playoffs'/'survival'/'midtable' (main.ts:1654-1660), yet deriveExpectation has `case 'promotion'`/`case 'relegated'` that can never fire (board.ts:180-182) — a 2nd-place promotion doesn't raise the bar; a relegation is under-tempered. fix: pass the actual promoted/relegated outcome (already computed, main.ts:1633-1634) into PriorFinish and have finishOf return 'promotion'/'relegated' on a tier move. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1654.
- [x] **PT-67 [FIXED] (audit: manager promotion/relegation): [low] board expectation decoupled from tier edges → unsatisfiable targets at top/bottom.** Expectation is prestige+prior-finish only (main.ts:1618): a Rookie at tier 10 is asked for 'survival' though relegation is impossible at the basement (nextSeason:1634 guards tier<TIERS); a high-prestige boss in tier 1 can be handed 'promotion' though tier 1 can't promote (guards tier>1). fix: clamp/relabel expectation by tier (treat 'promotion' as 'title' in tier 1; drop drop-zone framing in tier 10). Ref: client/src/main.ts:1618.
- [x] **PT-68 [FIXED] (audit: manager continental/world): [medium] World-Finals "final drama" line describes the SEEDED bracket final, not the star's actual final/exit.** `finalDrama = wcKnockoutDramaBlurb(wcSeed, wc.edition, wc.final)` (main.ts:1558) is shown under a report headed by the star's own run captioned "how the final played out" (:1554); for a run that ended before the seeded final (QF exit, or a title via a different final), it narrates a match the star was never in. fix: derive the drama from the star's decisive tie (their final if reached, else their elimination tie from wcRun), falling back to wc.final only for the pure-seeded view. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1558.
- [x] **PT-69 [FIXED] (audit: manager continental/world): [low-med] played World-Finals knockout shootout is a flat coin-flip while every other tie leans to the stronger side.** `resolveWorldCup` uses `< 0.5` (main.ts:1484) whereas `resolveContinental` uses `0.5 + (clubStr-oppStr)*0.03` (:1382) and `intl.ts knockout()` uses the same lean. fix: apply `0.5 + (starOverall - oppStrength)*0.03` to the WC played shootout. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1484.
- [x] **PT-70 [FIXED] (audit: manager continental/world): [low] the star's nation is drawn into Group A every edition.** `worldCup` builds `field=[myNation,...rivals]` then `field.forEach((n,i)=>groups[i%WC_GROUPS].push(n))` (intl.ts:114,120), so myNation (index 0) always lands in group 0; reports always print "Group A". fix: rotate the group offset by edition/seed (`groups[(i+edition)%WC_GROUPS]`). Ref: shared/src/intl.ts:114.
- [x] **PT-71 [FIXED] (audit: manager continental/world): [low] World-Finals verdict line is a single hardcoded template per finish; the seeded worldCupFinishBlurb bank sits dead.** `verdict` (main.ts:1549-1553) interpolates only nation/surname; the purpose-built `worldCupFinishBlurb`+`WC_FINISH_BLURB` (2 seeded variants/finish, intl.ts:253-283) is imported nowhere. Same repetition class as PT-57. fix: use `worldCupFinishBlurb(seed, edition, nation, finish)`. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1549.
- [x] **PT-72 [FIXED] (audit: manager continental/world): [low] a concluded World Finals report becomes permanently unreachable.** Once wcStage:'done'+wcSeen, `worldCupHtml` skips the run block (main.ts:1427) and `wcEditionDue` returns null (:1411); the group tables + bracket shown once in showWorldCup can never be reviewed and aren't kept in the trophy room. fix: keep a "View the last World Finals →" entry re-rendering from stored wcEdition/wcRun. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1411.
- [x] **PT-73 [FIXED] (audit: manager first-season): [medium] an injured bloodline star is force-started as a broken "ghost" player.** `starGuarded()` force-includes starId with no injury check (main.ts:1079-1090), but `availableClub()` excludes injured players (:604-609) and the match is built from it; `buildXI` does `club.players.find(id)!` on the missing star → `{...undefined}` (teams.ts:147), inserting a nameless NaN-overall player (10 men + a ghost). fix: in starGuarded, skip the force-include (or swap in the best healthy same-role sub) when `this.injured.has(starId)`. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1079.
- [x] **PT-74 [FIXED] (audit: manager first-season): [low-med] star FT spotlight and Player-of-the-Match are goals-only, so a playmaking/defensive masterclass is invisible.** `starLineFt` renders only if the star is in the goalscorer map (main.ts:3016-3020) and POTM is derived purely from `goalsBy` (:3021-3031, matchstats.ts:44-51) — a star with 3 assists or a keeper's clean sheet gets no callout, 0-0s show no POTM, and when your star blanks in a defeat the OPPONENT's scorer is crowned POTM on your own report. fix: fold assists (+ appearance/clean-sheet heuristic) into the star spotlight and POTM tie-break. Ref: client/src/main.ts:3016.
- [x] **PT-75 [FIXED] (audit: manager first-season): [low-med] rolling to the next season silently abandons an unfinished Continental Cup run.** `nextSeason()` resets contElig/contRound/contOut with no pending-tie guard (main.ts:1647), while "Next season →" appears as soon as the 18 league fixtures are done (:1243), independent of the continental ties shown alongside. Same silent-drop class as PT-38 (World Finals) but for the continental strand. fix: warn/confirm on sf-next-season when `contElig && !contOut && contRound<3` (or auto-sim the outstanding tie). Ref: client/src/main.ts:1647.
- [x] **PT-76 [FIXED] (audit: manager first-season): [low] the season-complete header celebrates only the title, not promotion.** The `done` summary appends "🏆 CHAMPIONS!" only when `t.pos===1` (main.ts:1243), so clinching promotion (2nd, tier>1) reads identically to a mid-table 2nd; the "⬆️ PROMOTED" reveal only surfaces on the NEXT season's screen (:1252). fix: detect promotion (`pos<=2 && tier>1`) in the done header and show a "⬆️ PROMOTED!" flourish there. Ref: client/src/main.ts:1243.
- [x] **PT-77 [FIXED] (audit: regression menu/settings): [medium] delete-save never removes the actual save data — only filters the fm_saves index.** `deleteSave` filters the index (main.ts:369-372); it never calls the save-layer/backend remove nor clears the per-handle keys (fm_mgr_/fm_tier_/fm_heir_/fm_ach_/fm_plan_/fm_bought_*/fm_retired_/onboarding). The confirm promises "gone for good — there's no undo," but the full save model (keyed by UUID token) + all dynasty/ach/tier state are orphaned. fix: also call the save layer's `backend.remove(save.token)` and delete every localStorage key suffixed with that save's handle. Ref: client/src/main.ts:369.
- [x] **PT-78 [FIXED] (audit: regression menu/settings): [medium] the family name is interpolated into innerHTML unescaped.** The live preview `<b>${raw}'s Club</b>` (main.ts:649), the save switcher `${s.name}` (:351), the delete confirm (:371), and the stored club name across the hub/table (:981) all inject the raw name; a name with `<`/`>`/`&` or a tag like `<b>x` (fits the 18-char maxlength) breaks layout or injects markup that then persists everywhere. fix: escape the family name (textContent or an escapeHtml helper) at every innerHTML interpolation. Ref: client/src/main.ts:649.
- [x] **PT-79 [FIXED] (audit: regression menu/settings): [medium] the pause-menu overlay uses a mismatched element id, defeating its own dedupe guard.** `openPauseMenu` removes `#pause-ov` but assigns the overlay `id='settings-ov'` (main.ts:469-470), so reopening ≡ Menu stacks a second identical overlay instead of replacing it. fix: make the removed id and the assigned id match (use `pause-ov` for both). Ref: client/src/main.ts:469.
- [x] **PT-80 [FIXED] (audit: regression menu/settings): [low-med] all-caps and multi-word family names aren't normalized.** Capitalization only uppercases char 0 in `startNewGame` (main.ts:497) and the live preview (:647): `raw.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+raw.slice(1)`. "MESSI" → "MESSI'S CLUB · the MESSI bloodline" (shouting); "de bruyne" → "De bruyne" (2nd word lowercase). Slips through the PT-40 lowercase fix. fix: title-case each word (lowercase the remainder, capitalize each word) for both preview and stored name. Ref: client/src/main.ts:497.
- [x] **PT-81 [FIXED] (audit: regression menu/settings): [low] ESC keydown listeners leak on any non-Escape dialog close.** The `onEsc` handler in openSettings (main.ts:446-447) and openPauseMenu (:488-489) only removes itself on the Escape path, never on ✕/backdrop dismissal, so each open→close cycle leaves a stale document-level keydown listener bound to a removed overlay's close(). fix: track onEsc and removeEventListener inside the shared close() so it's cleaned on every path. Ref: client/src/main.ts:446.

Root cause traced in code (single fix). No console errors. -->

- [x] **PT-52 [FIXED 521c8ea] (stage: player Breakthrough/senior re-pass): [high] story-ARC beats SOFT-LOCK the career — clicking either choice does nothing, freezing the save permanently and making the senior stage unreachable.** When the arc "🚪 THE EMPTY SEAT AT EVERY GAME" fired at Scholar (turn ~52), clicking "Agree to meet" or "Bin the letter" only applies a static amber highlight; the beat never resolves (verified across page reloads and a direct programmatic `.click()` — arc stays present, no advance, no console error). Root cause: `careerState()` returns EARLY for `st.phase === 'arc'` (`shared/src/tokens.ts:209-213`) with ONLY the arc snapshot, so the wrapper field `prospectId` is dropped; the click handler then calls `doCareerAct(s.prospectId=undefined, {type:'arc'…})` (`client/src/main.ts:2352`) → `api.careerAct(undefined,…)` throws `no such token` (caught → transient toast, no re-render). why it hurts: story arcs are a marquee senior feature and a judged item, yet the FIRST arc encountered bricks the entire save — the player cannot progress past it to Breakthrough at all; this alone blocked the whole assigned stage this pass · fix: in the arc branch of `careerState`, merge the arc payload onto the full state wrapper (include `prospectId`/`name`/`turn`/`totalTurns`) instead of returning the bare `st`, OR have `renderCareer` read `this.lastCareerState.prospectId`/the token id rather than `s.prospectId`. Ref: `shared/src/tokens.ts:206-213`, `client/src/main.ts:2352`, `client/src/api.ts:547-560`.
- [x] **PT-53 [FIXED 521c8ea] (stage: player Breakthrough/senior re-pass): [medium] during any story-ARC beat the career header blanks to "undefined" — reads "undefined · age 15" with an "undefined/undefined" turn counter.** On every render of the arc beat (fresh load included), the top bar shows literally `undefined · age 15 Scholar` and the progress pill `undefined/undefined` where the name and `turn/totalTurns` belong (elsewhere it reads "Enzo Rossi · age 15 · 52/202"). Same root cause as PT-52: the `st.phase === 'arc'` early-return in `careerState` (`shared/src/tokens.ts:209-213`) omits `name`/`turn`/`totalTurns`, and the header template consumes them raw (`client/src/main.ts:2228`: `${s.name} · age ${s.age}` and `${s.turn}/${s.totalTurns}`). why it hurts: even once the soft-lock is fixed, a story arc — the most cinematic beat — would still render the player's own name as "undefined", breaking the fiction at the emotional peak · fix: same fix as PT-52 (carry the wrapper fields into the arc state); no-name fallback in the header template as a belt-and-braces guard. Ref: `shared/src/tokens.ts:206-213`, `client/src/main.ts:2228`.
- [x] **PT-54 [FIXED] (stage: player Breakthrough/senior re-pass): [low-med] the "Breakout Year" season-event opener phrase repeats across BOTH scenario and outcome text — "Suddenly the name on everyone's lips" appeared in 3 of ~5 consecutive beats, "Riding the wave of a breakout season" in 2.** In a 5-turn Breakout-Year stretch: "Suddenly the name on everyone's lips" opened the Matchday-11 scenario, a matchday OUTCOME ("…in a rain-lashed youth-league tie, he nervelessly played A Composed First Touch…"), AND the training-ground scenario; "Riding the wave of a breakout season" opened both the Dockside scenario and the Defence-Splitter outcome. So the same seasonEvent opener is drawn for scenario openers and result flavour alike, from a small pool, producing near-back-to-back repeats. why it hurts: PT-9/PT-42 hardened the general banks, but the Breakout-Year seasonEvent opener still recycles within a handful of turns (and reusing a scenario opener as an outcome opener reads as a template seam) — the "every moment is a story" hook wears thin fast at exactly the breakout beat meant to feel special · fix: give the Breakout-Year (and other seasonEvent) openers a larger pool, track recently-used lines, and don't share one opener bank between scenario prompts and outcome prose. Ref: seasonEvent opener bank in `shared/src/narrate.ts`, consumed via `scenarioStory`/outcome narration.

- [x] **PT-47 [FIXED] (stage: player Youth/Scholar re-pass): [medium] the SUMMER FOCUS screen mixes repeatable "spend earnings" purchase tiles with the single summer-FOCUS tile that ends the screen — they look identical, and clicking a focus tile first advances immediately and strands your unspent coins with no warning or confirm.** The screen presents two card grids that look the same: "🌅 Between seasons … Steer your relationships" (8 focus tiles) and "💷 Treat yourself — or back the club — spend earnings (you have 1,020c)" (up to 9 purchase tiles). Observed behaviour differs completely with no signpost: clicking a SPEND tile ("A Private Tutor · 💷 500c") deducts coins in place (1,020c → 520c) and stays on the screen so you can buy more; clicking a FOCUS tile ("Rest & Recharge") immediately COMMITS and advances to the next screen. At age 13 I clicked the focus tile first (intending to spend afterwards) and was carried straight past the screen with all 304c unspent. why it hurts: a naive player who picks their relationship focus before spending silently forfeits an entire summer's earnings, and nothing tells them the two tile groups behave differently or that the focus pick is the "done" button · fix: visually separate/label the two groups (purchases vs the committing focus), and either gate the focus pick behind a confirm ("Spend more first?") or add an explicit "Continue" button so the focus tile isn't a hidden end-turn. Ref: summer-focus render in `client/src/main.ts`.
- [x] **PT-48 [FIXED] (stage: player Youth/Scholar re-pass): [medium] the Scholar SUMMER FOCUS legend omits the 🏠 Parents/Home meter and a ❤️ morale meter that its own tiles modify — no legend entry and no meter bar, so those deltas are unreadable (a regression from the age-13 legend, which listed 🏠 Parents).** At age 15 the legend row reads "⚡ energy · 🧑‍🏫 Coach · 👥 Teammates · 🎒 School · 🤝 Agent" — no 🏠 and no ❤️ — yet tiles hand out both: "📞 Call Home Every Night … 🏠+16", "🎁 Treat Your Parents … 🏠+14", "🚦 Driving Lessons … 👥+5 ❤️+4", "🛏️ Nicer Digs … ❤️+4", and the bottom meter row shows only ENERGY/COACH/TEAMMATES/SCHOOL/AGENT (no Parents, no Heart). The age-13 legend DID include "🏠 Parents", so Scholar dropped it while still using the icon. why it hurts: PT-10 added the legend to kill exactly this "icon soup", but at Scholar two live meters (Home and morale) have icons in no legend and no on-screen bar, so "🏠+16 / ❤️+4" read as undefined glyphs again · fix: include every icon a tile can affect (🏠 Parents/Home and ❤️ morale) in the legend and add their bars to the meter row for the Scholar stage. Ref: METER_ICON/METER_NAME + summer legend in `client/src/main.ts`.
- [x] **PT-49 [FIXED d7a9c47] (stage: player Youth/Scholar re-pass): [low-med] two cards with the IDENTICAL name and description appear together in the same life-event hand, distinguishable only by their tag pills.** Multiple LIFE EVENT hands (turns 27 and 28) dealt "Lean on the people around him / Not try to carry it alone / teamwork" AND "Lean on the people around him / Not try to carry it alone / teamwork composure" side by side — same title, same body, different tag sets. why it hurts: the card is the core choice object; seeing the literal same-named card twice reads as a duplicate/bug and makes the "which card matches?" decision needlessly confusing · fix: give tag-variants distinct names (or de-dupe the draw so two same-id/name cards can't be in one hand). Ref: off-pitch card pool + hand-draw in `shared/src/career.ts` / `narrate.ts`.
- [x] **PT-50 [FIXED d7a9c47] (stage: player Youth/Scholar re-pass): [low] a summer FOCUS effect doesn't match its described activity — "Study the Pros on VHS" (a solitary at-home activity) raises the 🧑‍🏫 Coach relationship by +10.** The tile reads "📹 Study the Pros on VHS — Rewinding the same clip over and over, trying to steal a touch you saw on the telly. ⚡-4 🧑‍🏫+10 · 👥+2" — watching videos alone at home logically shouldn't move your relationship with the coach (contrast the neighbouring "Impress the Coach — Extra sessions, first to arrive" → 🧑‍🏫+16, which fits). why it hurts: on a screen whose whole point is legible cause-and-effect, an effect that contradicts its flavour teaches the player to distrust the pills · fix: point VHS study at a trait/technique gain (or a smaller neutral meter) rather than the Coach relationship, or reword it as something the coach would actually notice. Ref: summer-focus option bank in `shared/src/career.ts`.
- [x] **PT-51 [FIXED d7a9c47] (stage: player Youth/Scholar re-pass): [medium] the "Dip in Form" debuff (PT-14) has effectively no perceptible recovery — its banner and "Low on confidence" scenario prefix persisted through ~18 consecutive Brilliant turns across TWO chapters and a summer Rest & Recharge, contradicting the pitch the entire time.** The banner "Dip in Form — You can feel it — the touch isn't quite there, and it's got into your head a bit." and the recolored openers ("Low on confidence, …" / "Stuck in a rut he can't explain, …") stamped every scenario from turn 22 (age 13 Academy, right after the summer) unbroken to turn 47 (age 15 Scholar entry) — through an unbroken run of "🎯 Right card / ⭐ Brilliant" grades AND a deliberately-chosen "Rest & Recharge" summer. PT-14 is marked FIXED ("a slump LIFTS as he strings good games together, +0.03/strong turn"), but at +0.03/turn the recovery is imperceptible over a normal sitting, so to the player the fix isn't present. why it hurts: the narration flatly contradicts what's happening on the pitch for a whole in-game year, turning the marquee prose monotonous and confusing at the exact stage this pass targets · fix: sharply raise the per-strong-turn recovery (and/or let a Rest summer clear most of a dip) so sustained Brilliant play visibly lifts the banner within a handful of turns; reopen/re-verify PT-14. Ref: form-momentum + dip-opener in `shared/src/career.ts` / `narrate.ts`.

- [x] **PT-42 [FIXED 2819bf8] (stage: player Grassroots re-pass): [high] scenario BODY text is machine-assembled by comma-splicing a situational fragment into a body sentence, producing ungrammatical run-ons — and PT-9's verbatim reuse is NOT holding (reopen PT-9).** Every scenario reads as two unrelated clauses jammed with a comma: "Terrified of being dropped for Saturday after one bad training session, a wet pitch, a heavy ball, and a game turning into a battle of wills" (turn 1); "A growth spurt has left him gangly and not quite sure where his own feet are, a tactical shape drill, and the staff want it drilled right" (turn 2); "Homework still not done and Mum already shouting for the car, a senior pro has pulled him aside for a word he didn't ask for" (turn 4). Worse, turn 6's Training Ground scenario was a verbatim splice of fragments already seen — "A growth spurt has left him gangly…" (=turn 2) + "a one-on-one against the reserve-team hardman — no easy reps today" (=turn 3) + "It's about the effort nobody notices until it's missing" (=turn 5) — i.e. the exact PT-9 symptom, within 6 turns. Two-in-one root cause: the fragment inserted mid-sentence is never grammatically joined, and the fragment pool is tiny so bodies recombine. why it hurts: for a text game whose whole hook is "every moment is a story", the marquee prose reads as broken/robotic AND repetitive on a first sitting · fix: give each scenario body a single coherent authored sentence (or join the fragment with proper syntax, not a comma), enlarge the body-fragment pool, and track recently-used fragments; reopen PT-9. Refs: `shared/src/narrate.ts` scenario/body banks, `shared/src/career.ts` makeScenario.
- [x] **PT-43 [FIXED 74f82e3] (stage: player Grassroots re-pass): [medium] off-pitch LIFE EVENT moments offer only football-action cards, so the "how does he handle it?" answers are nonsensical for the situation.** The "⚡ LIFE EVENT · OFF THE PITCH" turn ("Homework still not done and Mum already shouting for the car, a senior pro has pulled him aside…") asks "How does he handle it?" and then offers Stepover ("Take your man on with a stepover and a burst of pace"), Ice in the Veins, Defence-Splitter ("Slide a defence-splitting pass through the eye of a needle") and Lung-buster Run — pure on-pitch skill moves as ways to deal with homework/Mum/a senior pro. The demand was even "flair". why it hurts: it's a direct text-doesn't-match-the-situation break at a moment meant to add life-sim texture — playing "a defence-splitting pass" to answer a life event reads as a content bug · fix: give off-pitch/life events their own response-card pool phrased as choices (how he reacts, what he says/does off the pitch), or reframe those turns so football cards make sense.
- [x] **PT-44 [FIXED c8a6f9f] (stage: player Grassroots re-pass): [medium] a demand tag shown under "🎯 This calls for:" is graded "✗ Wrong card" when you play it — the primary(green)/secondary(yellow) pill distinction is never explained.** Multi-tag demands render every tag as an equal pill after "This calls for: … — play a card that matches" (e.g. turn 3 "creativity flair"; turn 5 "stamina creativity teamwork"), with only a green-vs-yellow colour hinting that one is the real match. Playing the yellow-listed tag scores as failure: turn 3 demand "creativity/flair", I played a **flair** card (Stepover) and got "✗ Wrong card ◦ Scrappy" — flair was literally listed under "This calls for:". why it hurts: PT-6 added the label but the colour semantics are undocumented, so a naive player rationally plays a called-for tag and is told they're wrong, teaching them to distrust the demand pill · fix: explain the colour (green = the match, yellow = partial/secondary) with a legend/tooltip, and don't brand a partial-credit tag "Wrong card" when it's shown as called-for and still develops him.
- [x] **PT-45 [FIXED 74f82e3] (stage: player Grassroots re-pass): [low-med] the rival news ticker prints the identical headline on every turn of the chapter.** "📰 Turner has just been picked up by a bigger local side." appeared unchanged on all 6 turns played (turns 1–6), including after the rival gap had swung both ways. why it hurts: the rival is sold as a living parallel career to chase; a frozen one-line headline makes his "story" feel canned and undercuts the "measured against him" hook · fix: rotate the rival-news line (tie it to his actual progress/gap swings, or draw from a pool the way PT-9 intends for flavor), and don't repeat the same headline turn-to-turn. Ref: rival-news render in `client/src/main.ts` rivalHtml / `shared/src/narrate.ts`.
- [x] **PT-46 [FIXED 2819bf8] (stage: player Grassroots re-pass): [low-med] Grassroots scenarios use pro-club language that clashes with the age-10 "jumpers for goalposts" framing.** The chapter is framed as "Grassroots · Jumpers for goalposts — muddy knees and big dreams" for a 10-year-old, yet the moments talk like a senior academy: "dropped for Saturday", "make the actual team, not just the bench", "a one-on-one against the reserve-team hardman", "the staff want it drilled right", "a senior pro has pulled him aside". why it hurts: the tonal mismatch between the whimsical grassroots billing and adult reserve-team vocabulary is jarring at the very first chapter and reads as fragments written for a later stage · fix: age-gate the scenario fragment banks so Grassroots draws child/park-football language (coaches, parents, school teams) and reserves "reserve team / staff / dropped for Saturday" for the later academy/senior chapters. Ref: `shared/src/narrate.ts` scenario banks keyed by chapter.

- [x] **PT-3 [FIXED — PERSONALITY is now a multi-variant, outcome-neutral bank picked at random (no more one recycled line; reads fine on win or loss). shared/src/narrate.ts.] (stage: player Grassroots): [high] outcome flavor text recycles within 6 turns — a workhorse player sees the identical voice line "No one on that pitch worked harder." on almost every strong/failed beat.** The prose sells the "every moment is a story" hook, but repetition breaks it fast: in a 6-turn run that one sentence appeared in 5 of 6 outcomes, plus "As a dog wandered across the far corner" ×3 and "Somebody, somewhere, will tell this story wrong in twenty years" ×2. Root cause: `PERSONALITY` is the only vocabulary bank that's one fixed string per id instead of an array, and it's appended ~60% of the time on triumph/dismal (`shared/src/narrate.ts:88-102`, used at `narrate.ts:321`). Fix: make `PERSONALITY` a `Record<string,string[]>` (like `PERSONALITY_ADV`) and `pick()` from it; also the same "worked harder" line reading as a compliment on a *dismal/Wrong-card* result is tonally off.
- [x] **PT-4 [FIXED — stars labelled as potential (tooltip); founding pedigree now shows 'first of the line · his heirs will inherit his pedigree' instead of a bare 0%. main.ts pedigreeText().] (stage: player Grassroots): [medium] the signed prospect shows "★★★★☆" and "pedigree 0%" side by side, which reads as a contradiction.** The academy explainer only defines pedigree ("the quality his bloodline passes down — a higher pedigree means a stronger natural ceiling"), so a new player sees 4 gold stars next to 0% and can't tell if the kid is great or worthless; the stars are never labeled or explained. Founding-generation pedigree is legitimately 0, but nothing signposts that stars = his own talent while pedigree = inherited bonus. Fix: label the stars (e.g. "talent"), and either hide "pedigree 0%" for the founding prospect or annotate it ("0% — you're the first generation; his kids inherit his pedigree").
- [x] **PT-5 [FIXED (lighter touch) — agent screen now reassures the first-timer ('no wrong pick; the tags play out slowly over the years'); kept all agents (variety) rather than trimming.] (stage: player Grassroots): [medium] the agent screen dumps 11 near-identical agents as the very first real decision, with effect pills that reference systems the player hasn't seen yet.** Choice overload before any card turn: pills like "more big-stage moments", "better card options at drafts", "higher transfer value" are meaningless to someone who has not yet played a matchday or a draft, and two 🃏 tiers ("slightly better drafts" vs "better card options at drafts") can't be ranked by a newcomer. Fix: trim the first-career agent list to ~3-4 clearly-differentiated options (or gate the rest behind later generations), and add a one-line "what this means" tooltip on the pill dimensions.
- [x] **PT-6 [FIXED — the demand is now labelled '🎯 This calls for:' with distinct highlighted pills (primary emphasised) + 'play a card that matches' hint, visually separate from card tags.] (stage: player Grassroots): [low-med] the scenario "demand" tag is a bare pill visually identical to the card tags, with no label telling the player it's the thing to match.** The moment's wanted trait (e.g. a lone "teamwork" pill, or "flair teamwork creativity") sits under the scenario prose in the same pill style as the four cards' tags, so a naive player may not realize matching that pill to a card tag is the core mechanic. It's also ambiguous when 3 demand tags appear but each card carries only 1-2 tags — which card "matches" isn't clear. Fix: prefix the demand pill(s) with a label ("They want:") and visually distinguish them from card tags; consider highlighting matching card tags.
- [x] **PT-7 [FIXED — the Player-tab role now carries a '· forming' cue (while traits still form) + a tooltip explaining position emerges from how you develop him.] (stage: player Grassroots): [low-med] a prospect pitched in the academy as "Looks like a forward" is classified DF on the Player tab after a couple of turns, with no explanation.** A new player who deliberately picks the "forward" prospect (Jude Cruz) is confused to open Player and find "DF · forming Ball-Winner · TAC 16". If position emerges from early card choices/stats that's a nice system but it's completely unsignposted, so it reads as a bug. Fix: either honor the academy descriptor as the starting position, or surface a note that his position/archetype is still forming from how you play him.
- [x] **PT-8 [FIXED — draft prompt now explains rarity = power (rare/epic develop him more when they fit) and that unpicked cards are discarded; reframed 'build your identity' as which moments he can answer. main.ts.] (stage: player Youth/Scholar): [medium] the between-seasons DRAFT sells "this is how you build your identity" but is a reject-2-of-5 pick (reject-1 in a breakout year), and the RARE/EPIC card pill is shown with zero explanation of what rarity does.** With `OFFER_SIZE=5` and `DRAFT_PICKS=3` (my draft was 4-of-5 due to a Breakout-Year bonus pick), the newcomer's only real decision is which one or two cards to cut — thin agency for a mechanic framed as identity-building — and a "RARE" card like Target Man carries a bare rarity pill (`cg-rarity`) with no tooltip, so there's no in-game signal that rarity means a bigger stat pull (it does — `shared/src/career.ts:59`). Fix: explain rarity on the card (tooltip: "rarer cards pull your stats harder"), and/or widen the offer pool so drafting is a real trade-off. Refs: `client/src/main.ts:2265-2268` (rarity pill), `:2189` ("build your identity"), `shared/src/career.ts:372-373` (OFFER_SIZE/DRAFT_PICKS).
- [x] **PT-9 [FIXED — expanded the openers (SETTINGS 6→11/chapter) and closers (REACTIONS 8→12/band) so flavour doesn't recycle within a chapter. shared/src/narrate.ts.] (stage: player Youth/Scholar): [medium] matchday flavor text still recycles verbatim within a chapter — PT-3's fix only touched the PERSONALITY bank, leaving the scenario-opener, scenario-prose and closer banks under-varied.** In a ~14-turn Grassroots run the opener "as a dog wandered across the far corner" appeared 4 times, the closer "the engine simply doesn't cut out" 4+ times, and whole scenario lines repeated word-for-word ("Homework still not done and Mum already shouting for the car…"; "A corner is about to be swung in, and the box is a scrum"). Root cause: small fixed pools relative to turns-per-chapter — only 6 Grassroots openers (`shared/src/narrate.ts:35`), fixed match/scenario banks (`narrate.ts:137`, `:182`), workhorse closers (`:95`). Fix: enlarge these pools and/or track recently-used lines to avoid near-term repeats (as PT-1's hand logic does for cards).
- [x] **PT-10 [FIXED — summer FOCUS now shows an icon legend (⚡ energy + the relationship meters in play) and each effect has a full-name tooltip. main.ts METER_NAME.] (stage: player Youth/Scholar): [low-med] the summer FOCUS screen renders each option's effects as bare icon-math (e.g. "🧑‍🏫+16 · 👥-3", "🧩 train teamwork") with no inline legend, mixing two unlabeled icon vocabularies in one pill row.** The first-timer HELP card explains relationships conceptually ("keep them healthy for better moments"), but never maps the glyphs (🧑‍🏫=coach, 🏠=family, 👥=teammates, 🎒=school) or distinguishes relationship-meter deltas from trait-training ("train teamwork"), so eight tiles of "+16/-3" icons still read as soup on first encounter. Fix: label the pills or add hover text on each glyph; visually separate "trains a trait" from "changes a relationship". Refs: `client/src/main.ts:2201` (effLabel), `:2217`, METER_ICON `:39` / TAG_ICON `:50`.
- [x] **PT-11 [FIXED — onboarding flags (fm_tut_done / fm_career_help_done) now keyed per-save (onbKey), so a new bloodline/New Game re-shows the onboarding instead of a global flag suppressing it forever. main.ts.] (stage: player Youth/Scholar): [low] the first-timer onboarding (the "HOW HIS CAREER WORKS" help card + tutorial coach-marks) is gated on GLOBAL localStorage flags, not per-save, so a returning browser never re-shows it.** `fm_career_help_done` / `fm_tut_done` are set once and persist across saves (`client/src/main.ts:2037,2240`); starting a brand-new bloodline/New Game does not reset them, so a player who samples the game once and later starts their "real" first dynasty gets no career explainer — and it silently suppressed the onboarding for this browser playtest until the flags were cleared by hand. Fix: reset these flags on New Game (or scope them per-save) so every genuinely-new career shows the explainer once.

- [x] **PT-12 [FIXED — big games now PLAY bigger, not just look it: stakes-2 moments demand ≥2 tags (stakes-3, three) so they test the deck instead of a single-tag gimme, AND get a distinct 'big occasion' payoff beat in the narration (stakes-3 already had the HUGE sequence). shared/src/career.ts + narrate.ts.] (stage: player Breakthrough/senior): [medium] big-stakes moments look escalated but play the same or EASIER than routine turns, and the outcome never acknowledges the stakes.** BIG GAME / ★ THE BIG ONE / 🔥 vs Turner / 🔥 SHOCK CALL-UP get distinct headers, borders and icons up front, but the card decision on them is frequently a SINGLE-tag demand (e.g. ★ THE BIG ONE vs Turner asked only for "flair"/"composure"), which is *easier* to satisfy than a routine 3-tag turn — so the mechanic gets looser exactly when the framing says it should tighten. The resolution then reuses the identical "Right card / Brilliant" chip and the same closer bank ("…the engine simply doesn't cut out. One in the eye for Turner.") as a meaningless reserve friendly. Net: tension is entirely front-loaded in the header; the choice and the payoff don't ratchet, so a naive player quickly learns the "BIG" tags are cosmetic. Fix: on big/★ matchdays raise demand complexity (more tags / tighter hand) and add stakes-aware outcome flavor + a visible reward (fame/boot/league swing) so the payoff matches the billing.
- [x] **PT-13 [FIXED (labeling, not balance) — the gap is YOUR score minus his; it now reads 'you're ▲ N clear' / 'you're ▼ N behind — reel him in' so it's unambiguous who leads. With good play you correctly pull clear. main.ts rivalHtml.] (stage: player Breakthrough/senior): [medium] the rival (Turner) gap balloons out of reach regardless of near-perfect play, contradicting the onboarding promise.** Onboarding sells the rival as beatable: "you're measured against him — out-develop him to pull ahead." Across a run graded ~90% Brilliant, the "ahead" counter went 74 → 157 → 313 → 497 (roughly doubling each season) while Turner's story escalated independently (first-team, "linked with a move across the city"). The gap never responded to my strong grades, so the rival comparison reads as cosmetic and unwinnable — demoralizing and directly at odds with the tutorial. Fix: tie the rival's rate to the player's actual performance so sustained strong play visibly closes the gap (and can overtake), making the "out-develop him" promise real.
- [x] **PT-14 [FIXED — form is now momentum: a slump LIFTS as he strings good games together (+0.03/strong turn) and its banner retires to 'Back to Form'; purple patches cool if results dip. No more 40-turn 'Low on confidence' stamp. shared/src/career.ts.] (stage: player Breakthrough/senior): [medium] the chapter-long "Loss of Form / Dip in Form" debuff has no in-chapter recovery and prefixes ~40 consecutive scenarios with "Low on confidence" despite unbroken success.** The banner ("Dip in Form — the touch isn't quite there…") persisted the ENTIRE Scholar chapter (~40 turns) through an unbroken streak of Brilliant results AND a summer "Rest & Recharge", only clearing at the chapter boundary — then re-triggered at the start of Youth Team and again at Breakthrough. Every scenario opener is recolored "Low on confidence, …", so the narration flatly contradicts what's happening on the pitch and turns monotonous. Distinct from the PT-9 flavor-pool work: this is a debuff-mechanic problem (no recovery path, wrong-signal framing), not bank size. Fix: let sustained strong play or a Rest summer visibly reduce/clear the dip, and stop stamping "Low on confidence" on turns the player is winning.
- [x] **PT-15 [FIXED — match-flavoured moment names (Derby Day/Cup Final) are now gated to MATCH scenarios only (rng draw preserved), so they no longer bleed onto training/life screens. shared/src/career.ts makeScenario.] (stage: player Breakthrough/senior): [low-med] match-only context tags bleed onto TRAINING GROUND and LIFE EVENT screens, where there is no match.** Repeatedly saw match-fixture phrases on non-match scenarios, e.g. "⚔ TRAINING GROUND … it's Derby Day", "⚔ TRAINING GROUND … it's Relegation Six-Pointer", "⚔ TRAINING GROUND … it's Cup Quarter-Final", and "LIFE EVENT … it's The Return to a Former Club". Describing a closed-doors drill or an off-pitch life moment as a "Derby Day"/"Cup Quarter-Final" is immersion-breaking and reads like a content bug. Fix: gate the match-context phrase bank to actual MATCHDAY scenarios; give training/life events their own context lines.
- [x] **PT-16 [FIXED — the between-season money decision is no longer identical every year: each archetype (money/fame/develop) now has 4 concrete variants, and rollOffer picks a different one per chapter (ids + character stable for replay safety). shared/src/career.ts.] (stage: player Breakthrough/senior): [low] the between-season "money now vs development" decision is the identical 3-option screen every single season.** Big-Money Move (+900c) / Boot & Kit Deal (+500c) / Stay & Develop (+120c) appears verbatim — same three options, same body text, same effect pills — at every chapter break (seen 4+ times: Academy → Scholar → Youth → Breakthrough). For a recurring headline choice framed as character-defining, zero variation plus no visible cumulative consequence (beyond the one-off coins) makes it read as filler by the second time. Fix: vary the offers/wording by chapter/fame level and surface how repeated greedy-vs-loyal picks compound over the career (personality, Fans meter, pedigree, or the Life-tab endorsements they clearly feed).

- [x] **PT-17 [FIXED — taking the reins now auto-picks a fresh XI from the merged squad and force-includes the bloodline star (drops the weakest starter if needed), so he's never benched at handoff. main.ts renderHandoff.] (stage: handoff): [medium] the moment after the handoff, the default match lineup BENCHES the very star the handoff just promised.** The take-the-reins card says "Sam Sato your man on the pitch", but the first SET LINEUP opens with an all-local XI (ratings 6–9; Key player shown as "Luis Santos (GK, OVR 9)") and Sam Sato (DF 16 💎, by far the best player) not selected in any of the 11 slots — you only get him in by clicking "Auto-pick best XI" or hand-adding him. A naive player can kick off their first-ever managed match with their bloodline star sat on the bench, directly undercutting the emotional beat. Root cause: `openLineup('match', …)` fills the draft XI from stale/default standing orders (soValid path) that predate the star joining the club, instead of guaranteeing the managed star is in the XI. Fix: force-include the managed star (or fall back to `autoPickXI`) whenever the standing XI omits him. Ref: `client/src/main.ts` openLineup() ~2571–2611.
- [x] **PT-18 [FIXED (HIGH) — succeed() snapshots the retiring pro as a legend (legendCardOf + saveLegacy, :g<gen> keyed) before reborn, so the Bloodline Tree / Hall of Legends populate. Regression-guarded in qa_offline_facade. client/src/api.ts.] (stage: succession): [high] the signature Bloodline Tree never populates — after a full retire→heir succession the Trophy Room still reads "No bloodlines yet · 🌳 0 bloodlines · ⭐ 0 legends".** I completed the whole loop (Sam Sato retired a 3-title club great; heir Leo Sato born, gen 1, pedigree 70%; "The Line Continues" achievement UNLOCKED; hub now shows the gen-1 heir), yet the Trophy Room's BLOODLINES section shows the empty-state ("develop a player… retire him to found a dynasty. Every generation after adds a link to the tree") and the "A Legend Retires" achievement stays 🔒 locked — sitting right next to the just-earned "🌳 The Line Continues", a visible self-contradiction. Root cause: `showTrophyRoom()` builds the tree from `api.legends()` → `legaciesFor()`, but the only writer to that store, `saveLegacy()` (`client/src/save.ts:216`), and `retirePlayer()` (`save.ts:195`) are DEFINED BUT NEVER CALLED anywhere in `client/src`/`shared/src` — so the manager-phase retirement/`api.succeed()` never records the founder as a legend or marks the token retired. The game's whole premise (see a bloodline "across the generations") has no payoff surface. Fix: on star retirement in `bringThroughHeir()`/`api.succeed()`, call `saveLegacy()` (and `retirePlayer()`) so the founder becomes a legend node and the Sam → Leo chain renders. Refs: `client/src/main.ts:1886` (showTrophyRoom), `client/src/api.ts:585` (legends), `client/src/save.ts:195,216`.
- [x] **PT-19 [FIXED — the retirement send-off now honours both careers ('once the heartbeat on the pitch, latterly the man in the dugout … two careers in the same shirt'). main.ts retireStar.] (stage: retirement): [low-med] the send-off honours only the MANAGERIAL record and forgets the playing career you actually played.** The retirement card frames it as the player hanging up his boots ("Sam Sato hangs up his boots") but credits only "11 seasons steering Marchetti's Club and 3 league titles" — the club's managerial honours. Nothing recaps HIS career: the years of card-turns you played from age 10, his 30+ international caps, POTM moments, or on-pitch grades. For a bloodline life-sim whose emotional weight comes from having lived his whole playing life, a farewell that only tallies the club's trophies feels impersonal at the exact beat it should feel most personal. Fix: lead the send-off with his playing legacy (career apps/goals/caps/POTM/legend rating) and treat the managerial titles as a secondary line.
