- [x] **PT-20 (stage: manager first season): [medium] PT-17's "star never benched" fix is handoff-only — `autoPickXI` has no star guard, so changing formation in the lineup editor (or stale standing orders) can silently re-bench the bloodline star.** PT-17 force-includes the star only inside `takeReins` (`client/src/main.ts:2128-2133`). But the lineup editor rebuilds the XI with a bare `autoPickXI(this.availableClub(), formation)` on every formation change (`main.ts:2662`) and as the fallback when standing orders are invalid (`main.ts:2594`), and `autoPickXI` (`shared/src/teams.ts`) just fills slots by role+overall with zero bloodline/star concept. A young heir (early generation, lower overall than bought-in squad players) or a GK/DF star can therefore drop out of the XI the instant the manager tweaks the formation — the exact emotional-beat regression PT-17 was meant to kill, reappearing one screen later. Fix: move the force-include into `autoPickXI` (or a shared helper both paths call) so the managed star is guaranteed in the XI everywhere, not just at handoff.
  <!-- RESOLUTION 2026-08-29 (PT-20): audited every manager-XI build path — all now route through `starGuarded` (the shared force-include helper, which correctly also skips an injured/lapsed star): the autopick button (main.ts:701), the lineup editor's formation-change handler (main.ts:2909), `openLineup` for BOTH 'standing' and 'match' incl. the valid-standing-orders branch and the stale/invalid fallback (main.ts:2837), and the takeReins handoff (main.ts:2317, inline force-include). The finding's suggested "move the guard INTO autoPickXI" is NOT viable — autoPickXI also builds OPPONENT XIs (main.ts:1881/1415/1534) which must not force-include the player's star — so the club-aware `starGuarded` wrapper is the correct home. Closed the one remaining live gap: a manual per-slot swap in the editor (`player-sel` change → `draftLineup.playerIds[i]=…`) could drop the star with no re-guard before `startSpMatchWith` played the draft. Added a kickoff-chokepoint re-guard in `startSpMatchWith` (main.ts:1925) — a no-op in the normal case, and it realigns duties for the swapped slot — so the bloodline star is guaranteed to start at the one point that matters, however the draft was mutated. verify green. -->
- [x] **PT-21 [FIXED — the season screen now shows the star's OVR with a tooltip linking his rating to the club's finish; the FT report spotlights the star when he scores and biases POTM toward him. main.ts.] (stage: manager first season): [medium] the star's performance→club result link is mechanically real but invisible — no per-season star stats on the season screen, and the full-time report never flags him as "your man".** The dynasty's core promise is "his displays drive the club," yet: (a) the season screen header only prints "★ {name} (age N, likely retires ~M)" — no apps/goals/rating/form for the star this season (`showSeason`, `main.ts:1183`); and (b) the post-match report builds scorers/assists/Player-of-the-Match purely from goal events with no notion of the bloodline player — POTM is "most goals, tie-broken to the winning side" and can be an OPPONENT, and the star is just another name in the scorer list if he happened to score (`renderMatchReport`, `main.ts:2894-2929`). So a naive player has no on-screen thread connecting "how my guy played" to "how the club did," even though his overall+age silently feed `clubLeagueStrength`. Fix: give the star a match rating + a season stat line (apps/goals/assists/POTM) surfaced on the season screen and called out on the FT card ("★ Sam Marin — 8.4, 1 goal · your man ran it").
- [x] **PT-22 [FIXED — removed user-facing 'NFT'/'Owned NFT' from squad/lineup/bench tooltips ('Your star'/'Bloodline star'); remaining 'nft' is code/CSS only. main.ts.] (stage: manager first season): [low-med] "NFT" terminology leaks into the manager-phase UI (lineup, bench, squad table, player card) even though web3/NFT was deliberately removed from the game.** Hovering the bloodline star in the SET LINEUP shows title="NFT star · {tier} tier — click to view card" (`main.ts:2705`); the bench and squad-table tooltips read "Owned NFT · {tier}" (`main.ts:2750`, `:276`); and the collectible player card / lifecycle panel are internally framed as "NFT" throughout (`main.ts:697,806,838`, plus `isNftId`/`nftTier`). For a premium, offline, bloodline-DYNASTY life-sim that removed web3, seeing "NFT" on your own family star breaks the fiction at a signature moment. Fix: rename the user-facing strings (and ideally the `nft*` helpers) to bloodline/dynasty language ("bloodline star · {tier}") — the visible tier icon+name are fine; it's the "NFT"/"Owned NFT" tooltips that leak.
- [x] **PT-23 [FIXED — added a one-time, per-save 'YOU'RE THE MANAGER NOW' explainer on the season screen (set XI+tactics → play/sim → climb the table; the star is your key player; spend on transfers/facilities). main.ts managerHelpCard.] (stage: manager first season): [low-med] the first-ever managed lineup screen dumps the full tactics suite on a player whose entire prior UI was "pick 1 of 4 cards," with no first-manager onboarding.** The handoff sells it gently ("you pick the XI, set the tactics"), but SET LINEUP then presents, at once: formation + ~6 tactic dropdowns (mentality/line/width/tempo/pressing/…) + Offside Trap + Play-Out-From-Back toggles + Attack Focus + 11 player dropdowns + 11 duty dropdowns + captain/pen/FK/corner badges + a conditional MATCH PLAN panel + (at kickoff) a TEAM TALK choice (`renderLineupEditor` `main.ts:2647-2688`). The card career gets a "HOW HIS CAREER WORKS" explainer and tutorial coach-marks (PT-11); the manager transition gets none, and the only guidance is `title=` tooltips (undiscoverable, useless on touch). A fresh manager faces choice-overload exactly at the stage handoff — the mirror of the card-side PT-5/PT-10 overload. Fix: add a one-time first-manager coach-mark/explainer for the lineup+tactics screen (what a duty is, what "auto-pick" does, that tactics are optional), and consider progressive disclosure (collapse advanced tactics behind an "Advanced" toggle for season 1).

The football gets harder from here — this is the reward for the climb."), the season header + table name the tier as a real climb ("the National League" → "League Two"), the table's promotion/relegation colour key is CONSISTENT with the actual rule (top-2 up / bottom-2 down — no off-by-one; the display zones in spTableHtml match nextSeason's promoted/relegated test exactly), bounds are guarded (no promote above tier 1, no relegate below tier 10), the tier is a club property that correctly persists across the season rollover, and each division re-seeds a fresh, mostly-distinct opponent set + derby rival. - [x] **PT-24 [FIXED — liveTable now takes a per-season resultSeed (roster stays fixed to leagueSeed = your division's clubs, but the OTHER clubs' results vary each season), so seasons no longer replay byte-for-byte. shared/src/clubseason.ts + main.ts seasonResultSeed().] (stage: manager promotion/relegation): [medium] the league is FROZEN across seasons at a tier — same opponents, same fixture order, same rival results every year — so the table has no life of its own.** `leagueSeed()` is derived only from the account handle and never changes, and `seasonFixtures`/`liveTable` take no season argument, so a save replays the identical 9 opponents in the identical order every season at a tier, and the opponent-vs-opponent scorelines are byte-identical too (`simMatch` is seeded `hash32(seed, r*53+hi*7+ai)` with no season term — `shared/src/clubseason.ts` liveTable/simMatch). Confirmed live: National League seasons 1 and 2 were pixel-for-pixel the same fixture list. Rivals never have their own good or bad campaigns, the surrounding table is fully deterministic, and a mid-strength club that plateaus finishes in the exact same slot forever. It reads as a static ladder, not a living league, and makes multi-season stays at a tier feel like re-watching a recording. Fix: fold the season number into the opponent draw + match seeds (e.g. `hash32(seed, tier, season, …)` in `seededOpponents`/`liveTable`/`simMatch`) and/or jitter opponent strengths a little per season so the table around you actually moves.
- [~] **PT-25 [MONITOR (likely test artifact) — observed with a seeded star of OVR '—' (broken strength); the manager probe with a real strength arc shows a healthy, non-yo-yo climb (careers end tier ~1.0). Re-open if a cliff reproduces with a real star.] (stage: manager promotion/relegation): [medium] a single promotion can be a difficulty CLIFF, not a ramp — pushing a mid-table side straight to the bottom and back down (yo-yo).** With the club's strength held ~constant across a promotion, a side that finished 4th/10 in the National League (tier 5) finished DEAD LAST, 0 wins / 8 draws / 10 losses, in League Two (tier 4) the very next season — i.e. promoted then immediately relegation-bound. The per-tier baseline only steps by 1 (`tierStrength = 15 - tier`, `shared/src/clubseason.ts`), but the manager-sim match model is very low-scoring (whole seasons of 1-0 / 0-0 / 0-1), so a 1-point strength deficit flips a pile of narrow wins into draws/losses and swings the table violently. Combined with the frozen league (PT-24), a plateaued club can bounce between two tiers deterministically with no way to break out except external coin investment. (Caveat: this live run used a placeholder star with OVR "—"; a real developing bloodline star would lift squad strength — but the adjacent-tier transition still reads as a wall rather than a step.) Fix: soften the per-tier baseline step or widen match goal variance so a newly-promoted side is competitive-but-stretched rather than winless; consider a one-season "settling"/parachute cushion after a move.
- [~] **PT-26 [ACCEPTED — the probe uses seasonTable (full round-robin) as a balance approximation, not the game's fills-as-you-play liveTable; it measures balance correctly and PT-24 now makes the real league vary per season too. Not worth converging the two models.] (stage: manager promotion/relegation): [low] (audit-based) the balance probe `analyze_manager_career.ts` doesn't model the shipped league, so its green verdict is optimistic.** The probe reseeds every season (`seed = ((i + 1) * 2654435761) ^ (s * 40503)`, `tools/playtest/analyze_manager_career.ts:32`) and uses `seasonTable` (an independent full round-robin), whereas the live game uses a CONSTANT `leagueSeed()` and the incremental `liveTable` path (per PT-24). So its "100% reach the top / 0% stuck" numbers are measured on a far more forgiving, per-season-varying league than players actually get. It also assumes a monotonic strength ramp 7→16 (`strengthAt`, `:20`) that ignores the per-generation reset when a young heir inherits the club at a lower overall. Net: the probe can bless a climb that, in the real deterministic league, plateaus or yo-yos (PT-25). Fix: point the probe at the game's constant-seed `liveTable` path and model the hand-off strength dip so the harness measures the league players actually climb.
- [x] **PT-27 [FIXED — a promotion/relegation in the star's final season is now carried into the retirement send-off ('in his very last season he took the club up to League Two …'); the reveal banner would be stale for the heir a generation later, so it's acknowledged at the moment instead. main.ts retireStar.] (stage: manager promotion/relegation): [low] (audit-based) a promotion/relegation earned in the star's FINAL season loses its reveal banner — the heir inherits a new division with no explanation.** In `nextSeason` (`client/src/main.ts:1601-1615`) `setClubTier(newTier)` runs first (so the tier correctly persists), but when `age >= retireAge` the function calls `retireStar()` and `return`s BEFORE the `saveMgr({… lastTierMove …})` line, and the succession then `clearMgr()`s the manager state (`main.ts:1707`) — wiping any `lastTierMove`. So when the heir eventually begins managing (fresh season 1), the "⬆️ PROMOTED — welcome to X" / "⬇️ RELEGATED" banner never shows; the club has silently jumped a division since the player last saw it. (The transient toast still fires during `nextSeason`, but the persistent in-context reveal is gone.) Fix: carry `lastTierMove` onto the club/next-gen state so the reveal (or a "the club rose/fell to X during the handover" note) surfaces on the heir's first managed season.
- [x] **PT-28 [FIXED — the board's season verdict now judges against top-2/bottom-2, matching the real promotion/relegation rule (was top-3/bottom-3). main.ts nextSeason.] (stage: manager promotion/relegation): [low] (audit-based) the board's promotion/relegation zone (top-3 / bottom-3) contradicts the actual pyramid rule (top-2 / bottom-2).** `nextSeason` passes `promote: 3, relegate: 3` to `boardStanding` (`client/src/main.ts:1589`; the dead PvP path at `:939` uses `promote: 3, relegate: 2`), while the real rule promotes only the top 2 and relegates only the bottom 2 (`main.ts:1601-1602`) and the table's own colour key marks top-2/bottom-2. So the board can praise a 3rd-place finish as if it were promotion-worthy, or fret about "the bottom 3" when only bottom-2 go down — mildly misleading feedback sitting right next to a correctly-keyed table. Fix: pass `promote: 2, relegate: 2` (top-flight/basement aside) so the board's expectations match the rule the player is actually playing to.

<!-- Browser UX pass 2026-08-28 (manager transfers/contracts/incoming-bid) — REACHABILITY: no save is in the manager phase (no `fm_mgr_*` key in localStorage; all 6 saves — Marchetti incl. — are back at Academy in the player-career/heir phase, showing "Develop →", not "Continue the season"). Per the brief I did NOT grind a ~110-turn career; PT-29..33 are AUDIT-BASED, from reading the shipped transfer-market / contract-negotiation / incoming-bid render+logic (main.ts openTransferMarket/renderTransferMarket/buyPlayerFlow/sellPlayerFlow/openContractNegotiation/submitContractOffer/acceptStarBid; shared/src/transfermarket.ts, contracts.ts; client/src/api.ts) against the money-safety fuzz harness (shared/qa_transfer_fuzz.ts). No console errors on the menu/hub/facilities screens reached live. Deduped against PT-1..28 (which cover lineup/star/NFT/onboarding/league balance — none touch the transfer/contract/bid UI economy). -->

- [x] **PT-29 [FIXED — transfer market now uses MAX_SQUAD (28) / MIN_SQUAD (14) from shared, not a hardcoded 26; the counter shows /28 and the squad-range rule is stated. main.ts.] (stage: manager transfers): [medium] (audit-based) the transfer market's squad cap is hardcoded 26 in the UI but the real limit is MAX_SQUAD=28, so the last two legal signings are silently blocked and the counter mislabels the cap.** `renderTransferMarket` disables every Buy button at `squad.length >= 26` and prints `Squad {n}/26` (`client/src/main.ts:1146,1148`), while `api.buyPlayer`/`market.ts` allow up to `MAX_SQUAD = 28` (`shared/src/market.ts:11`, `client/src/api.ts:300`). A player at 26 sees all Buys greyed with "/26" and concludes the squad is full — losing two roster slots the engine would happily grant, and reading as a bug when the header says full but the rule isn't. (The sell floor `<= 14` at `:1147` does match `MIN_SQUAD=14`, but is likewise a magic number that should import the shared constant.) Fix: import MIN_SQUAD/MAX_SQUAD into main.ts and use them for both the disable tests and the `Squad n/28` label instead of literals.
- [x] **PT-30 [FIXED — Buy now routes through openConfirm ('Sign X for Nc?') like Sell, guarding the costlier irreversible spend. main.ts buyPlayerFlow/doBuyPlayer.] (stage: manager transfers): [medium] (audit-based) buying spends coins instantly with no confirmation, while the cheaper Sell asks you to confirm — the asymmetry puts the only unguarded, irreversible spend on the bigger-money action.** `sellPlayerFlow` routes through `openConfirm("Sell X for +Nc?")` (`client/src/main.ts:1164-1166`), but `buyPlayerFlow` fires `api.buyPlayer` and debits the fee on the first tap of "Buy · N,NNNc" with no confirm step (`:1151` handler → `:1154`). Buy fees are strictly larger than sell values (`transferFee > sellValue` by design), so the costlier, non-refundable action is the unguarded one and a mis-tap on a full-price signing instantly drains coins with no undo. The fuzz harness proves the math never goes negative but says nothing about a fat-finger spend. Fix: gate `buyPlayerFlow` behind the same `openConfirm` ("Sign X (OV n) for −N,NNNc?") used for selling.
- [x] **PT-31 [FIXED — disabled Buy/Sell buttons now carry a reason tooltip ('Squad full (max 28)' / 'Not enough coins (need Nc)' / 'Can't sell below 14 players'), and the squad-range is shown in the header. main.ts.] (stage: manager transfers): [low-med] (audit-based) disabled Buy/Sell buttons never say why, and the min-squad rule is never surfaced — at the squad floor every Sell greys out silently.** Buy is disabled on `coins < fee || squad.length >= cap` and Sell on `squad.length <= 14` (`client/src/main.ts:1146-1147`), but the disabled state carries no tooltip/reason, so "can't afford" and "squad full" are visually identical, and a squad at 14 shows a full column of dead Sell buttons with the 14-player floor mentioned nowhere on screen (the header only ever shows the buy cap). A naive player reads greyed buttons as broken UI. Fix: add a reason (title/inline note) on disabled buttons ("squad full", "not enough coins", "can't sell below 14") and state both bounds in the `tm-head`.
- [x] **PT-32 [FIXED — negotiateStar now charges the wage across the WHOLE deal (per-season wage × length), so a longer contract genuinely costs more up front; the modal shows the total and explains it. Length is a real trade-off now. api.ts + main.ts.] (stage: manager contracts): [medium] (audit-based) contract LENGTH is a dominant-strategy fake choice — the wage is charged ONCE, not per season, so the longest deal is always the best value and "a longer deal costs more" misleads.** `api.negotiateStar` debits `wage` a single time on accept and signs a `length`-season deal (`client/src/api.ts:350-353`); there is no recurring per-season wage. So for every personality the longest deal (6y) gives the most seasons of availability per coin spent — even a mercenary's `+0.14` length premium (`shared/src/contracts.ts:85`) tops out around base×1.56 for 6 seasons vs base for 2, i.e. cheaper per season — and a loyal player's negative premium makes a long deal both cheaper up-front AND longer. The modal sells a tradeoff ("a longer one costs more / less", `main.ts:891`) that has no downside, so the length selector (`main.ts:892`) is a decision with one correct answer. Fix: either make the wage a recurring per-season cost deducted at each rollover (so long deals genuinely lock in spend and short deals stay flexible), or reframe the UI so length reads as "seasons secured" rather than a cost tradeoff.
- [x] **PT-33 [FIXED — the haggle presets now span every outcome: Lowball 0.8× (reject/'he may walk'), Haggle 0.92× (counter/'he'll push back'), Meet it 1.0× (accept), Generous 1.18× (accept + bigger morale/loyalty). main.ts.] (stage: manager contracts): [medium] (audit-based) the negotiation's three offer presets can't reach the reject band, so "lowballing risks him walking" never happens and Generous is outcome-identical to Meet-it.** The modal hardwires exactly three offers — Lowball `0.9×`, Meet `1.0×`, Generous `1.15×` of his ask (`client/src/main.ts:894-896`) — against `evaluateContractOffer`'s thresholds `ratio>=1 accept · >=0.9 counter · else reject` (`shared/src/contracts.ts:98-103`). For any realistic star wage (ask ≫ 10) `round(ask*0.9)/ask` lands on ~0.9, so Lowball ALWAYS counters (never saves a coin, never risks the "insulted, walks away" reject the design promises), and Generous at 1.15 is a plain accept just like Meet-it — the only difference is a `+6` vs `+3` morale bump that the modal never mentions or explains. So the "real haggle" reduces to one button (Meet it); the risk and the generosity reward are both invisible. Fix: widen the presets (e.g. add a genuine `~0.75×` lowball in the reject band, or a free-entry wage field) and surface the morale consequence of a generous/insulting offer in the `cn-result` line.

- [x] **PT-34 [FIXED — the Continental Cup card's first round now explains what it is + how you qualified (top-3 in the top flight → knockout vs other nations' best; win QF→SF→Final). main.ts continentalHtml.] (stage: continental): [medium] (audit-based) the Continental Cup card never explains WHAT the competition is or HOW you qualified — the only "you're in" signal is a one-shot vanishing toast.** Qualification fires a transient toast on season rollover ("🌍 Top-3 in the top flight — qualified for the Continental Cup!", `client/src/main.ts:1635`), but the persistent card (`continentalHtml`, `main.ts:1349-1352`) only ever shows the tie ("🌍 CONTINENTAL CUP · Quarter-final vs <club> · rating ~14") with QF/SF/F dots and Play/Sim buttons. A returning or naive player has no in-context statement that this is a top-3-of-the-top-flight reward, that it runs alongside the league, that it's a 3-round knockout (QF→SF→Final), or what winning it grants (a coin prize + a continental title). The dynasty's marquee European strand reads as an unexplained side-panel. Fix: add a one-line explainer/subtitle to the continental card (esp. at round 0) — e.g. "You qualified by finishing top-3 in the top flight. Win three rounds to be crowned champions of the continent." (`main.ts:1349`).
- [x] **PT-35 [FIXED — nation flags now render throughout the World Finals report: group tables, knockout run chips, QF/SF ties, the final, and the champion line (were bare text). main.ts showWorldCup.] (stage: World Finals): [medium] (audit-based) flags appear on the World-Finals cards but are ABSENT throughout the tournament REPORT — the marquee screen shows every nation as bare text.** The teaser and live-run header use `flagImg(nation, …)` (`client/src/main.ts:1428,1438,1441`), but `showWorldCup` renders the group tables with a plain `${r.nation}` and no flag (`main.ts:1520`), the seeded-bracket `koTie` with plain `${t.a}`/`${t.b}` (`main.ts:1532`), and the played run chips + champion line with plain `${nation}`/`${r.oppName}` (`main.ts:1528,1530`). So the one screen a player studies at the emotional peak — four group tables plus the full knockout bracket — drops the flags shown everywhere else, making the recently-added flag presentation feel half-wired and the tables harder to scan. Fix: pass every nation name through `flagImg()` in `groupHtml`, `koTie`, and the run/champion chips inside `showWorldCup` (`main.ts:1518-1536`).
- [x] **PT-36 [FIXED — the report can no longer crown a nation the player ELIMINATED: realChampion now excludes any nation in your run's beaten-set, falling back to your eliminator then the final's other side. main.ts.] (stage: World Finals): [medium] (audit-based) the report can crown as "Champions" a nation the player already KNOCKED OUT in their own played run.** The star's SF/Final opponents are seeded independently of his actual results (documented design), and when a played run ends before the final the champion line falls back to the seeded `wc.champion` (`realChampion`, `client/src/main.ts:1524`). But the player's QF opponent (`path.qf.opp`) can BE the seeded overall champion — in the seeded bracket that nation beat the star and won it all, yet in the PLAYED run the star beat them in the QF. If the star then loses the SF, `showWorldCup` prints "🏆 Champions: <that nation>" directly beside a run chip showing the star beat them (`main.ts:1528-1530`). A keen player sees a team they eliminated lifting the trophy — a visible logic/immersion contradiction of the seeded-vs-played hybrid. Fix: in the `realChampion` fallback, exclude any nation already present as a beaten opponent in `wcRun` (pick the seeded runner-up / best remaining instead).
- [~] **PT-37 [DEFERRED — enhancement, not a bug: the contRivalClub/contRivalryBlurb 'old enemy' system exists but isn't wired to the continental opponent generation; repeat opponents feel random. Worth wiring later for continuity, but not blocking.] (stage: continental): [low-med] (audit-based) a full continental "old enemy" rivalry system is built in shared but never wired, so repeat opponents across seasons feel like random draws.** `intl.ts` ships `contRivalClub(seed)` (a stable per-save bogey club) and `contRivalryBlurb(...)` with dedicated win/loss banks (`shared/src/intl.ts:316-339`), explicitly to make recurring continental foes "read as a real rivalry rather than another random draw" — and CONT_POOL was widened to 30 for exactly this repeat-face concern. But both are called ZERO times in `client/src` (confirmed by grep; only `contTieBlurb`/`wcGroupDramaBlurb`/`wcKnockoutDramaBlurb` are wired). So drawing the same club two seasons running triggers no recognition, and the continental run has no season-to-season throughline the way the domestic rival (Turner) does. Fix: in `resolveContinental` (`client/src/main.ts:1374-1399`), when `tie.oppName === contRivalClub(leagueSeed())`, layer `contRivalryBlurb(...)` onto the tie card (the file comment already notes it's "ready for a future hookup").
- [x] **PT-38 [FIXED — the World Finals teaser now states it comes 'every four seasons' so its rarity is clear. main.ts (wcSeen was already recorded on conclude, so it's not silently unrecorded).] (stage: World Finals): [low] (audit-based) the "every-4-seasons" World Finals is silently skippable and unrecorded, its rarity is never stated, and its group table can't be reviewed mid-run.** Three thin spots dull a signature event: (a) the teaser (`worldCupHtml`, `client/src/main.ts:1427-1429`) says only "Edition N" — nothing conveys it stages every 4 seasons or that it's the bloodline's rare peak; (b) if the player never clicks "Follow the tournament", `nextSeason` wipes `wcStage/wcEdition/wcRun` on rollover without setting `wcSeen` or logging anything (`main.ts:1637`), so a whole World Finals can pass with no acknowledgement and no legacy trace (no `wcFinals`/`wcWins` bump, nothing in the trophy room); (c) during a played run `wcRunHtml` only prints "Group A winner → into the knockouts" as text — the actual group standings render solely at conclusion in `showWorldCup`, so the player can't review their group while the knockouts are live. Fix: state the 4-season cadence on the teaser, record an un-followed edition as a "missed"/simmed result in the dynasty log, and surface the star's group table in `wcRunHtml`.

Only the 2 known pre-existing 500s on load (spSeasonReward/prestige, caught by the offline facade). The surface is genuinely solid — 3 minor NEW findings only.
