read for premise-vs-age plausibility; the 111 non-youth (saga/crisis/triumph/relationship/signature/offpitch) arcs in
that set were read in full. VERIFIED CORRECTLY GATED, do not touch: (a) the entire 211-arc youth-* childhood library
— every keyword hit in it is a false positive (a parent's "car park"/"garage"/"drives him", "bar" = the crossbar,
"dead" = a dead ball, "flat" = a flat pass, "adopt" = adopts a formation, "a session" = a training session), and its
one alcohol context (youth-fam-uncle, youth-season-presentation-night, youth-season-sign-on-again) is an adult in a
pub or a grassroots presentation night, not the player drinking; (b) the 12 gates raised by the previous romance pass
— no arc reachable before turn 46 now contains the player's own girlfriend/proposal/live-in partner; (c) the age-17
professional-life arcs that are genuinely realistic and should stay: youth-first-money (15, boot money),
tri-young-player (46, agent interest), crisis-red-card (47), crisis-training-bustup (46), crisis-form-slump (50),
crisis-own-goal (50), crisis-horror-tackle (50), crisis-referee-war (50); (d) the age-18/19 Breakthrough block
(saga-*, sig-*, off-nightlife 60, off-crypto 60, off-betting-sponsor 60, saga-loan-abroad 57, tri-promotion 57) —
these assume a young senior pro, which is what turn 57-65 is. -->

- [x] **PT-1100 (audit: age-appropriateness): [critical] six arcs put the PLAYER behind the wheel of a car years before he can legally hold a licence, including the reported 15-year-old driving through the night.** `rel-family-illness` (minTurn 26 = age 15) resolves with "He drives through the night and holds her hand while the machines beep" and later "He moves them nearer the ground and starts driving over on days off"; `rel-grandparent` (minTurn 26 = age 15) has him "wires the old house with help and drives the long road to her"; `rel-pen-pal-fan` (minTurn 38 = age 16) "He drives over after training with a signed shirt"; `rel-foreign-signing` (minTurn 41 = age 16) "drives him to viewings and the bank"; `tri-first-senior-goal` (minTurn 23 = age 14) and `rel-football-dad` (minTurn 24 = age 15, "He's a grown professional now") both narrate a driving-age adult life around him. The UK minimum is 17 (turn 46) and a provisional licence-holder cannot drive alone. why it hurts: it is the single most visible break in the life-sim's core promise — the game states the player's age on screen and then narrates him doing something he is legally years away from, which reads as a bug in the very system the mode is named after. fix: `rel-family-illness` 26→86, `rel-grandparent` 26→86, `rel-pen-pal-fan` 38→86, `rel-foreign-signing` 41→86 (all four also spend adult money — a flat, a house, a bank run); `rel-football-dad` 24→66 (maxTurn is 85, so 86 is illegal for this arc); `tri-first-senior-goal` 23→46, the earliest turn a senior debut is plausible. Ref: `shared/src/storyarcs/relationship.ts`, `shared/src/storyarcs/triumph.ts`.
- [x] **PT-1101 (audit: age-appropriateness): [critical] a full gambling-addiction-and-match-fixing arc and an alcohol-dependency arc can both open on a 17-year-old, and a 15-year-old is written drinking pints.** `crisis-gambling` (minTurn 47 = age 17) runs "stakes that stopped being funny", debts "bigger than a fan earns in a year", then a fixer offering to "clear the whole debt in one night, all he has to do is see a yellow card at a set minute" — this is the arc the playthrough flagged, and it is reachable by a Scholar who cannot legally hold a betting account. `crisis-drink-spiral` (minTurn 47 = age 17) opens on "a grainy phone clip of him being poured out of a bar at four in the morning". `rel-sibling-dream` (minTurn 29 = age 15) resolves with "he resists the urge to solve it and just listens over a pint, night after night". why it hurts: this is the class of content that draws an age-rating problem on a Steam-bound premium title — a minor protagonist gambling, being groomed into match-fixing, and drinking are not "mature themes", they are a rating escalation, and they land on a player the UI is simultaneously labelling as 15. fix: `crisis-gambling` 47→86 (age 21 — an established pro with money to lose and a career worth corrupting), `crisis-drink-spiral` 47→66 (age 19, past the legal 18), `rel-sibling-dream` 29→86 (its brother is "released at thirty" and the player funds his coaching badges). Ref: `shared/src/storyarcs/crisis.ts`, `shared/src/storyarcs/relationship.ts`.
- [x] **PT-1102 (audit: age-appropriateness): [critical] the player is made a bereaved best friend's godfather and an expectant father at 18, both chronologically impossible for the age.** `rel-godchild-coach` (minTurn 54 = age 18) opens "His late best friend's son — his godson, ten years old and football-mad" and asks him, "as the closest thing to a dad the boy has", to advise the mother on a residential academy place: an 18-year-old would have been named godfather at eight and buried a peer who already had a child. `rel-becoming-father` (minTurn 57 = age 18) is the pregnancy-and-delivery-room arc, which the previous romance pass left behind because it never uses the word "girlfriend" — the guard's blind spot exactly. why it hurts: the godfather arc is the one the playthrough named as making no sense at all, and it is worse than implausible — it hands a teenager a dead peer, an orphaned child and a guardianship decision in a game that has just been showing him doing his GCSEs. fix: `rel-godchild-coach` 54→104 (age 23, the only band where the arithmetic works), `rel-becoming-father` 57→86 (age 21, and in line with the marriage/partner gates the previous pass set). Ref: `shared/src/storyarcs/relationship.ts`.
- [x] **PT-1103 (audit: age-appropriateness): [high] eleven relationship/triumph arcs are gated at turns 23-45 (ages 14-16) but every one of them is written for an established senior professional with a wage, a flat and a transfer history.** The whole 23-45 block is mis-scaled: besides PT-1100's drivers, `rel-best-mate` (26 = age 15) has a club bidding for his teammate who "turns up at the flat at midnight" before an airport goodbye; `rel-hometown-friend` (29 = age 15) has "the flash car", a mate selling stories to a paper, and the player clearing a five-figure debt; `rel-kitman` (35 = age 16) calls him "the club's biggest name" and has him top up a 63-year-old's pension; `rel-estranged-parent` (27 = age 15) is emotionally age-neutral but assumes a stand full of paying fans. None of these can happen to a Scholar in school-age football. why it hurts: it is the same failure mode across a whole category — the rescale to 120 turns divided every gate down but the prose still describes a 25-year-old, so the Scholar chapter reads as somebody else's career spliced into a child's. fix: `rel-best-mate` 26→66, `rel-hometown-friend` 29→86, `rel-kitman` 35→86, `rel-estranged-parent` 27→46. Ref: `shared/src/storyarcs/relationship.ts`.
- [x] **PT-1104 (audit: age-appropriateness): [high] seven arcs cast the player as the dressing room's senior figure, elder statesman or top earner while he is 17-19 and still in the youth setup.** `rel-chairman` (minTurn 50 = age 17) has a billionaire owner offering "a future on the board when the boots come off" over yacht dinners; `crisis-wage-cut` (63 = age 19) addresses him as "a top earner and a leader" whose lead the squad follows; `crisis-dressing-mutiny` (63) needs "one of the biggest voices in the room" to sign a revolt against the manager; `saga-new-money` (63) calls him "one of the old guard" becoming "a relic in his own home"; `crisis-legend-feud` (60) has a club icon spending a whole season calling him "an insult to the shirt"; `rel-troubled-youngster` (63) and `rel-academy-keeper` (60) both cast him as "the senior man" mentoring a teenager two years his junior. why it hurts: seniority is the mode's progression fantasy — handing it out during Youth Team flattens the arc of the whole career, and it produces the specific absurdity of a 19-year-old being told he is past it. fix: `rel-chairman` 50→86, `crisis-wage-cut` 63→86, `crisis-dressing-mutiny` 63→86, `crisis-legend-feud` 60→86, `rel-troubled-youngster` 63→86, `rel-academy-keeper` 60→86, `saga-new-money` 63→104. Ref: `shared/src/storyarcs/relationship.ts`, `shared/src/storyarcs/crisis.ts`, `shared/src/storyarcs/saga.ts`.
- [x] **PT-1105 (audit: age-appropriateness): [medium] five more arcs assume a career length, a rehab history or a fortune the player has not had time to accumulate.** `rel-old-teammate-reconcile` (minTurn 60 = age 19) is built on "a decade ago a title slipped away" and "ten years of not speaking" — i.e. a falling-out at nine; `rel-pundit-expartner` (57 = age 18) has an old roommate "retired into punditry" telling him he is "past it… time to walk away"; `rel-physio-loyalty` (46 = age 17) needs a career-ending knee plus "eighteen months of dawn rehab" already behind him and a glamour transfer in front; `rel-coach-passing` (46 = age 17) is fine as grief but resolves with him endowing a boys' club "with a pitch and a coach's wage"; `crisis-new-league` (50 = age 17) is a "big move, big fee" transfer abroad, which FIFA Article 19 forbids under 18. `rel-mentor-kid` (57 = age 18) mentors a fifteen-year-old for two seasons. why it hurts: none of these are unsafe, but each one silently tells the player his career is longer or richer than the save says it is, which undercuts the one-linear-save premise. fix: `rel-old-teammate-reconcile` 60→104, `rel-pundit-expartner` 57→104, `rel-physio-loyalty` 46→86, `rel-coach-passing` 46→86, `crisis-new-league` 50→66, `rel-mentor-kid` 57→86. Ref: `shared/src/storyarcs/relationship.ts`, `shared/src/storyarcs/crisis.ts`.
- [x] **PT-1106 (audit: age-appropriateness): [medium] the validator's age guard only catches the player's own adult ROMANCE, so driving, gambling, alcohol and guardianship regressions ship silently — extend it to four tiered rules.** `tools/playtest/validate_arcs.ts:59-72` runs one regex (`ADULT_WORDS`) against one threshold (`CHILD_TURN_LIMIT = 46`); every arc in PT-1100 to PT-1102 passes it today. Replace the single check with a rule table — `const AGE_RULES = [{ limit: 46, name: 'DRIVING', re: /\bHe (?:drives|drove)(?! off\b)|\b(?:drives|drove) (?:the long road|through the night|himself)|(?<!who )(?<!Who )\b(?:drives|drove) (?:him|her|them) to |\bhis own car\b|\bbehind the wheel\b|\bdriving licence\b|\bpassed his (?:driving )?test\b/ }, { limit: 56, name: 'GAMBLING', re: /\b(?:gambling problem|a gambler|match-fix\w*|spot-fix\w*|fixing (?:a|the) match|betting (?:app|firm|slip|shop|sponsor)|chased? (?:a |the )?loss|the bookies|an accumulator|integrity line|doubles? the stakes)\b/i }, { limit: 56, name: 'ALCOHOL', re: /\b(?:a pint|pints|poured out of a bar|the drink has|drink(?:ing)? problem|last orders|VIP table|nightclub|vodka|a lock-in)\b/i }, { limit: 86, name: 'GUARDIANSHIP', re: /\b(?:godson|goddaughter|godfather|godchild|his newborn|going to be a dad|the delivery room|legal guardian)\b/i }]` — keeping `ADULT_WORDS` as-is alongside it. The thresholds are the legal lines the bands imply: 46 = age 17 (driving), 56 = the earliest turn the player is 18 (gambling, alcohol), 86 = age 21 (guardianship). Case-sensitive `He drives` and the `(?<!who )` lookbehind are load-bearing: they are what keep "It drives him hard" (`youth-doubt-too-hard`), "before he drives off" (`youth-coach-leaves`), "the man… drives him for another two years" (`youth-fam-car`) and "the little cluster who drove him to every trial" out; dropping "a session", "champagne", "his child" and "his son" is what keeps the 211-arc youth library, `tri-promotion`, `off-nightlife` and `rel-estranged-parent` out. why it hurts: the previous romance pass fixed twelve gates and left a guard so narrow it certified a library containing a 15-year-old drink-driving-adjacent life and a 17-year-old match-fixing plot as "no adult material reachable before age 17". fix: add the table above and loop it exactly as `ADULT_WORDS` is looped. Verified against the current 414-arc library it produces **exactly 9 hits and zero false positives** — `rel-family-illness`, `rel-grandparent`, `rel-pen-pal-fan`, `rel-foreign-signing` (DRIVING), `crisis-gambling` (GAMBLING), `crisis-drink-spiral`, `rel-sibling-dream` (ALCOHOL), `rel-godchild-coach`, `rel-becoming-father` (GUARDIANSHIP) — i.e. it is green the moment PT-1100 to PT-1102 land, and red until then. Ref: `tools/playtest/validate_arcs.ts:59`.

<!-- Match-commentary text-repetition audit 2026-08-29 (code + simulation only, no browser). Scale simulated: 200 matches through the REAL MatchEngine via `runMatch` (two BASE_QUALITY-6 `makeClub` squads, Balanced/Balanced) for the event census; 40 of those matches re-run through a faithful port of `Game.pushTicker`/`flushMove`/`cidx` to render the ACTUAL commentary strings and count per-bank draws, per-line load and back-to-back identical picks, in both Full and Key modes. Measured baseline: 783.1 engine events per match -> 699.2 rendered lines per match in Full mode, of which only 221.7 are distinct (31.7% unique) with the single worst string repeating 39.9 times per match on average (worst observed 52). Key mode: 128.2 lines, 65.7 distinct (51.2% unique). Season figure at 18 league rounds + cup ties (~20 matches): ~14,000 commentary lines a season, ~140,000 across a ten-season manager career. VERIFIED FINE, do not touch: the `goal` bank (8 lines, 3.02 draws/match, and its `lastGoalIdx` guard measured 0 back-to-back repeats in 40 matches), `kickoff`/`injury`/`free_kick`/`yellow`/`penalty`/`red` (all under 1.2 draws/match), and the goal-line's tally/game-state/assist decoration, which is what keeps the biggest moment reading fresh. Determinism note that applies to EVERY fix below: all commentary text is client-side in main.ts and selected by `cidx`, a pure hash of (matchSeed, event index, salt) that never touches `makeRng` — enlarging any bank, or replacing `cpick` outright, consumes ZERO engine rng draws and cannot perturb a (seed + action list) replay's result. -->
- [x] **PT-1200 (audit: match commentary): [critical] the passage-of-play lead is picked with an index that is CONSTANT for 82% of passages, so one phrase prints ~190 times in a single match.** `flushMove` calls `cpick(pool, seq.length + touches, 7)` — but `seq = uniq.slice(-4)`, so for a two-touch passage the index is always 2+2=4, and measured over 40 matches **82.3% of passages are exactly two touches** (14.4% three, 2.7% four): only 6 distinct index values exist all match, and `cidx(4, 4, 7)` is fixed per seed. Result: the 4-line short bank is drawn **229.4 times per match (4,589 a season)**, 57.4 draws per line, and **76.1% of consecutive draws return the identical line** — measured worst single match, "Neat from Beta12's Club:" 48x and the same lead on 189 of 230 passages. The 3-line long bank (`touches >= 7`) is the opposite problem: it fired **2 times in 40 matches (0.05/match)**, i.e. content nobody sees. why it hurts: passages of play are the single most numerous line type in the feed (33% of everything rendered), so this one broken index is most of what makes the commentary read as a loop rather than a match. fix: stop deriving the index from the content — pass a monotonic per-match passage counter and select with a strided coprime picker (`pickByTurn`-style, `shared/src/narrate.ts:268`) so consecutive passages walk the whole bank; enlarge the short bank 4 -> 20 lines and the long bank 3 -> 7, and drop the long-bank gate from 7 touches to 5 (measured 3.2% of passages, ~7 a match) so it is actually reachable. No rng-draw-count change (client-side `cidx` only). Ref: `client/src/main.ts:3654`.
- [x] **PT-1201 (audit: match commentary): [critical] the `loose_ball` bank is 4 lines carrying 201 draws a match, and half of them contain no player name, so they render as just 3 fixed strings.** Measured over 40 matches: **201.1 draws per match (4,023 a season), 50.3 uses of every line in every match**, with 24.1% of consecutive draws returning the same line. Two of the four lines ("The ball breaks loose ${zone}." and "Scrappy — it pinballs around ${zone}.") interpolate only the zone word, so ~100 draws a match collapse onto **6 possible strings**; measured worst single match: "Scrappy — it pinballs around in the final third." **52 times** and "The ball breaks loose in the final third." 47 times. This is the 28x-repeat the playthrough capture flagged, only worse in a longer match. why it hurts: it is the second-most-drawn bank in the game and the most literally identical, and it appears in the calm stretches where the player is actually reading rather than watching the score. fix: enlarge 4 -> 18 lines (arithmetic: 201 draws / 18 ≈ 11 per line per match, and with a no-repeat picker no two adjacent), and make every new line interpolate `${p}` so the player name carries the variation the zone word cannot; 18 drafted below. No rng-draw-count change. Ref: `client/src/main.ts:3712`.
- [x] **PT-1202 (audit: match commentary): [high] the two tackle banks hold 7 lines between them against 118 draws a match, and the 3-line press bank repeats back-to-back a third of the time.** Measured: `tackle_won` with `zone === 'att'` (the press bank, 3 lines) draws **73.3 times a match — 24.4 uses of each line, every match, 1,466 a season** — and **32.7% of consecutive draws are the identical line**; the non-att tackle bank (4 lines) draws 44.3 a match, 11.1 per line, 27.0% back-to-back. Both are ⚡/🦵-prefixed so they are visually clustered in the feed, which makes the recurrence more obvious than the raw count suggests. why it hurts: the press lines are the ones that sell a tactical identity ("we win it high") and they are the ones a player sees twenty-four times a match in three phrasings, which reads as the game having one idea about pressing. fix: press bank 3 -> 12 lines (73/12 ≈ 6 per line per match), tackle bank 4 -> 14 (44/14 ≈ 3 per line); apply the same strided no-repeat picker as PT-1200. No rng-draw-count change. Ref: `client/src/main.ts:3704`.
- [x] **PT-1203 (audit: match commentary): [high] in "Key" mode — the shorter, more-read feed — the whole surface is 6 chance lines plus 5+5 shot lines, and 48.8% of it is duplicate text.** Key mode renders **128.2 lines a match of which only 65.7 are distinct (51.2% unique)**; the `chance` bank (6 lines) is drawn **58.2 times a match (1,163 a season), 9.7 uses of each line per match**, `shot_missed` (5 lines) 28.8 draws / 5.8 per line, `shot_saved` (5 lines) 27.1 / 5.4, with 15-19% of consecutive draws identical. Measured worst Key-mode strings in one match: "Alpha6's Club carve it open — Ryo Rossi with a sight of goal!" 10x, "Felix Rossi shifts it onto his stronger foot…" 10x. Separately the 4-line counter-attack chance bank fired **0 times in 200 matches** with Balanced tactics both sides (it needs the opponent on `attackPush >= 9` or `lineShift >= 3`), so a quarter of the chance vocabulary is invisible to most players. why it hurts: Key mode is the setting a player picks when they want to read rather than skim, and it is where the banks are thinnest relative to their draw rate. fix: `chance` (open play) 6 -> 18 lines, `shot_missed` 5 -> 14, `shot_saved` 5 -> 14 (each lands ~2-3 uses per line per match); keep the counter bank at 4 but note it as tactics-gated content in the design doc rather than treating it as live vocabulary. No rng-draw-count change. Ref: `client/src/main.ts:3696`, `client/src/main.ts:3699`.
- [x] **PT-1204 (audit: match commentary): [high] `cpick` is a plain hash index with no no-repeat guard anywhere except the goal line, so every bank can and does print the same phrase twice running.** `cidx(len, idx, salt)` (main.ts:3561) avalanche-hashes (matchSeed, eventIndex, salt) and takes it modulo the bank length — uniformly distributed, with no memory of the previous pick, so the back-to-back collision rate is the expected 1/n. Measured across 40 matches: **flow_short 76.1%** (the PT-1200 index bug), press 32.7%, corner **44.7%** (2-line bank), sub 21.3%, tackle 27.0%, loose_ball 24.1%, foul 20.4%, momentum 23.7%, shot_saved 18.4%, shot_missed 18.2%, chance 17.2%. Only `goalLine` guards itself (`lastGoalIdx`, measured 0 repeats in 121 goals) — the codebase already has the right tool twice over in `pickByTurn` (`shared/src/narrate.ts:268`) and `stridedIdx` (`shared/src/career.ts`), both of which walk a whole coprime-strided cycle before any entry recurs. why it hurts: adjacent identical lines are far more visible than the raw repeat rate — two "Turnover! X robs him in midfield." on consecutive rows is what a reader registers as broken, and it happens roughly a quarter of the time in the busiest banks. fix: replace `cpick(arr, idx, salt)` with a strided picker holding a per-bank draw counter reset each match — `arr[stridedIdx(arr.length, bankTurn[salt]++, matchSeed, 7)]` — which is deterministic, replays identically, and guarantees no repeat inside a full cycle of the bank. Pure client-side selection change, zero rng draws. Ref: `client/src/main.ts:3561`.
- [ ] **PT-1205 (audit: match commentary): [medium] the feed emits 699 lines a match, 72% of them from three banks, which is why enlarging banks alone cannot fix it — the engine's flow throttle is over-generous in the final third.** Measured per match: 330.1 `pass` events (collapsing into 230.6 passage lines), 197.9 `loose_ball`, 116.8 `tackle_won` — **so 503 of 699 rendered lines (72%) come from `flow_short` + `loose_ball` + `press`/`tackle`** while the 21 other banks share the remaining 196. `MatchEngine.flow` throttles by zone at `att: 5s / mid: 11s / def: 16s`, and the 5s attacking gap is what generates the pile-up (all four worst-repeating strings measured end "in the final third"). Two further banks are effectively dead content: `woodwork` (3 lines) and `fatigue` (4 lines) fired **0 times in 200 matches** at BASE_QUALITY-6 squads (woodwork needs shot quality > 0.5, fatigue needs fitness < 0.56 with workrate > 10). why it hurts: 14,000 lines a season is more text than any bank size can keep fresh, and the density is highest exactly where the player is trying to follow a move. fix: widen the attacking gap 5 -> 8s and midfield 11 -> 14s in `MatchEngine.flow` (measured effect: roughly a third fewer flow lines, ~470/match total), which halves the per-line load on the three fat banks before any new writing; separately re-check the woodwork/fatigue gates against real squad quality. IMPORTANT determinism note: `flow()` is add-only and consumes no rng, so changing the gap does not alter the rng draw count or any match RESULT — but it does change the `events` array a replay renders, so a mid-season saved match will commentate differently after the change (scoreline identical). Ref: `shared/src/engine.ts:67`.
- [ ] **PT-1206 (audit: match commentary): [medium] the corner, momentum and substitution banks are 2-4 lines each against 3.5-9 draws a match, and corners are the worst repeat rate in the game.** Measured: `corner` **2 lines, 3.52 draws/match (70 a season), 44.7% of consecutive draws identical** — the highest back-to-back rate of any bank once PT-1200's index bug is excluded; `momentum` 4 lines, 8.95 draws/match (179 a season), 23.7% back-to-back; `sub` 3 lines, 6.00 draws/match (exactly 6 every match — the bench logic is deterministic, so the player sees three phrasings for six changes every single game, 120 a season). why it hurts: substitutions and momentum swings are narrative beats the player caused, so re-using the same sentence for them undercuts the one moment the commentary is describing their decision. fix: `corner` 2 -> 8 lines, `momentum` 4 -> 10, `sub` 3 -> 8; with PT-1204's strided picker a 6-draw bank of 8 lines cannot repeat at all within a match. No rng-draw-count change. Ref: `client/src/main.ts:3723`, `client/src/main.ts:3591`, `client/src/main.ts:3725`.

     DRAFTED COMMENTARY LINES (audit 2026-08-29) — additions in the existing house

  ── flow_long (sustained passage, main.ts:3655) — 3 existing + 4 new = 7 ──
  `${team} have not given it up — ${touches} passes: `
